<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-47939579" data-id="47939579" data-tags="Open Source|Career|Programming" data-score="3448" data-comments="1029" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939579" data-time="1777405492">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mitchellh.com/writing/ghostty-leaving-github" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mitchellh.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mitchellh.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mitchellh.com/writing/ghostty-leaving-github" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ghostty is leaving GitHub</span><span class='zh'>Ghostty 将离开 GitHub</span></a><span class="dom">mitchellh.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ghostty is leaving GitHub, signaling concerns about platform bind and the viability of OSS workflows outside centralized hosting.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostty 将离开 GitHub，凸显了在中心化托管之外维持开源工作流的挑战与风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ghostty is leaving GitHub, signaling concerns about platform bind and the viability of OSS workflows outside centralized hosting. The move prompts reflection on dependencies, portability, and how maintainers plan long-term collaboration without GitHub’s ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostty 将离开 GitHub，凸显了在中心化托管之外维持开源工作流的挑战与风险。此举引发对依赖关系、可移植性以及维护者在无 GitHub 生态的情况下如何长期协作的思考。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Career' style='--c:#2dd4bf'>Career</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>3448</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939579' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1029</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47865868" data-id="47865868" data-tags="Startups|Business" data-score="2250" data-comments="761" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865868" data-time="1776875365">
<a class="thumb" href="https://wheelfront.com/this-alberta-startup-sells-no-tech-tractors-for-half-price/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#fb7185' data-tag='Startups'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=wheelfront.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>wheelfront.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://wheelfront.com/this-alberta-startup-sells-no-tech-tractors-for-half-price/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Alberta startup sells no-tech tractors for half price</span><span class='zh'>阿尔伯塔初创以半价销售无科技拖拉机</span></a><span class="dom">wheelfront.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An Alberta startup is selling traditional, no-tech tractors at half the price of modern models, targeting farmers who value simplicity and repairability.</span><span class='zh'>一家阿尔伯塔初创企业以半价销售无科技的拖拉机，瞄准看重简单与易修的农民。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An Alberta startup is selling traditional, no-tech tractors at half the price of modern models, targeting farmers who value simplicity and repairability. By removing electronics, they cut upfront costs and maintenance headaches, challenging mainstream ag-tech pricing and support models.</span><span class='zh'>一家阿尔伯塔初创企业以半价销售无科技的拖拉机，瞄准看重简单与易修的农民。去除电子系统后，制造与维护成本下降，这一做法可能影响主流农业科技的定价和售后模式。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>2250</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865868' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>761</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47687273" data-id="47687273" data-tags="Programming" data-score="2244" data-comments="477" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687273" data-time="1775638422">
<a class="thumb" href="https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/cover_hu3f66e25b7571f7e32d40f355f31a2ca9_56928_1200x630_resize_box_2.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='piechowski.io' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Git commands I run before reading any code</span><span class='zh'>在读代码前我会执行的 Git 命令</span></a><span class="dom">piechowski.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Before diving into a codebase, I run a concise set of Git commands to map the repo’s history, structure, and hotspots.</span><span class='zh'>在进入代码库前，作者先执行一小组 Git 命令来快速梳理历史、结构与关键区域。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Before diving into a codebase, I run a concise set of Git commands to map the repo’s history, structure, and hotspots. The routine surfaces remotes, recent commits, file changes, and likely entry points, helping you jump straight to meaningful parts.</span><span class='zh'>在进入代码库前，作者先执行一小组 Git 命令来快速梳理历史、结构与关键区域。这个流程能快速显示远端、最近提交、改动文件以及潜在入口点，帮助开发者直接进入重点代码。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>2244</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687273' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>477</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47840219" data-id="47840219" data-tags="Business|Hardware" data-score="2157" data-comments="1301" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840219" data-time="1776717572">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/04/tim-cook-to-become-apple-executive-chairman-john-ternus-to-become-apple-ceo/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/04/tim-cook-to-become-apple-executive-chairman-john-ternus-to-become-apple-ceo/tile/Apple-John-Ternus-Tim-Cook_lp.jpg.og.jpg?202604202226' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='apple.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/04/tim-cook-to-become-apple-executive-chairman-john-ternus-to-become-apple-ceo/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>John Ternus to become Apple CEO</span><span class='zh'>约翰·特诺斯将任苹果公司 CEO</span></a><span class="dom">apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple has announced a leadership transition with Tim Cook moving to executive chairman and John Ternus named CEO, signaling a planned succession.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布高层交接，蒂姆·库克将出任执行董事长，约翰·特诺斯接任 CEO。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple has announced a leadership transition with Tim Cook moving to executive chairman and John Ternus named CEO, signaling a planned succession. Ternus, a veteran hardware and product operations leader, would steer Apple’s product roadmap and hardware/software integration in the next era. The move underscores Apple’s emphasis on continuity and a hardware-centric strategy.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布高层交接，蒂姆·库克将出任执行董事长，约翰·特诺斯接任 CEO。特诺斯长期担任硬件/产品运营负责人，接任后将引领苹果的产品路线与硬件/软件协同。此举突显苹果在保持连续性和以硬件驱动战略方面的长期规划。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>2157</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840219' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1301</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47949027" data-id="47949027" data-tags="Programming" data-score="2091" data-comments="675" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949027" data-time="1777473259">
<a class="thumb" href="https://zed.dev/blog/zed-1-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://zed.dev/img/post/one-point-zero/thumbnail.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='zed.dev' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://zed.dev/blog/zed-1-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zed 1.0</span><span class='zh'>Zed 1.0 发布</span></a><span class="dom">zed.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zed 1.0 marks the first stable release of the Zed language, accompanied by its tooling and documentation.</span><span class='zh'>Zed 1.0 标志着 Zed 语言的首个稳定版本及其工具链的发布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zed 1.0 marks the first stable release of the Zed language, accompanied by its tooling and documentation. The release signals readiness for broader adoption in systems programming and showcases a focus on safe, expressive syntax and productive tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Zed 1.0 标志着 Zed 语言的首个稳定版本及其工具链的发布。此举表明该语言准备迎接更广泛的应用，强调安全、可读性强的语法和高效的开发工具。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.newyorker.com/magazine/2026/04/13/sam-altman-may-control-our-future-can-he-be-trusted" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sam Altman may control our future – can he be trusted?</span><span class='zh'>萨姆·奥特曼可能掌控我们的未来——能被信任吗？</span></a><span class="dom">newyorker.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sam Altman&#x27;s influence over AI policy and development raises questions about the balance of power and accountability in tech leadership.</span><span class='zh'>萨姆·奥特曼在AI领域的影响力引发关于科技领袖权力与责任平衡的讨论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sam Altman&#x27;s influence over AI policy and development raises questions about the balance of power and accountability in tech leadership. The piece challenges readers to weigh optimism about rapid innovation against safeguards for society, privacy, and equity. Understanding who shapes AI&#x27;s trajectory matters for engineers and policymakers alike.</span><span class='zh'>萨姆·奥特曼在AI领域的影响力引发关于科技领袖权力与责任平衡的讨论。文章促使读者权衡对快速创新的乐观态度与对社会、隐私和公平的保障之间的取舍。理解谁在塑造AI的未来，对工程师与政策制定者都至关重要。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://api-docs.deepseek.com/news/news260424" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/img/docusaurus-social-card.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='api-docs.deepseek.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://api-docs.deepseek.com/news/news260424" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepSeek v4</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek v4</span></a><span class="dom">api-docs.deepseek.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepSeek v4 continues to push long-context capabilities, delivering more memory-efficient retrieval and scalable context processing for large language models.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek v4 在长上下文能力上进一步提升，提供更高效的检索和可扩展的上下文处理，便于大模型在海量文档上进行推理而不耗费过多资源。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepSeek v4 continues to push long-context capabilities, delivering more memory-efficient retrieval and scalable context processing for large language models. The update targets practical deployments that need to reason over very long documents without blowing through resources.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek v4 在长上下文能力上进一步提升，提供更高效的检索和可扩展的上下文处理，便于大模型在海量文档上进行推理而不耗费过多资源。此版本面向实际部署场景，改善长期上下文的可用性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.stepsecurity.io/blog/axios-compromised-on-npm-malicious-versions-drop-remote-access-trojan" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Axios compromised on NPM – Malicious versions drop remote access trojan</span><span class='zh'>Axios 在 NPM 上被劫持：恶意版本投放远程访问木马</span></a><span class="dom">stepsecurity.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Malicious Axios releases popped up on npm, embedding a remote access Trojan in certain versions.</span><span class='zh'>npm 上出现恶意的 Axios 版本，包含可对受影响环境进行远程访问的木马。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Malicious Axios releases popped up on npm, embedding a remote access Trojan in certain versions. The attack highlights the ongoing supply-chain risk for widely used libraries, and operators should audit dependencies, pin safe versions, and monitor for unusual network activity after updates.</span><span class='zh'>npm 上出现恶意的 Axios 版本，包含可对受影响环境进行远程访问的木马。此类供应链攻击再次暴露了对广泛使用库的依赖风险，建议锁定版本、进行依赖审计，并在更新后监测异常网络活动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bryankeller.github.io/2026/04/08/porting-mac-os-x-nintendo-wii.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I ported Mac OS X to the Nintendo Wii</span><span class='zh'>我把 Mac OS X 移植到了任天堂 Wii</span></a><span class="dom">bryankeller.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A personal experiment porting Mac OS X to the Nintendo Wii, outlining the core challenges of cross-hardware porting and what it reveals about software portability.</span><span class='zh'>作者进行了将 Mac OS X 移植到任天堂 Wii 的个人尝试，概述了跨硬件移植的核心挑战以及软件可移植性在极端约束下的意义。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A personal experiment porting Mac OS X to the Nintendo Wii, outlining the core challenges of cross-hardware porting and what it reveals about software portability. The write-up highlights bootstrapping, driver gaps, and the kinds of trade-offs required when attempting to run a modern OS on a legacy console.</span><span class='zh'>作者进行了将 Mac OS X 移植到任天堂 Wii 的个人尝试，概述了跨硬件移植的核心挑战以及软件可移植性在极端约束下的意义。文章聚焦引导启动、驱动缺口，以及在旧世代主机上运行现代操作系统所需的取舍。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A report suggests LinkedIn collects data from installed browser extensions, raising privacy and data-use questions for a platform that handles professional identity and recruitment.</span><span class='zh'>有报道指 LinkedIn 会收集已安装的浏览器扩展数据，引发关于隐私和数据使用的质疑，尤其是在处理专业身份与招聘的场景中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A report suggests LinkedIn collects data from installed browser extensions, raising privacy and data-use questions for a platform that handles professional identity and recruitment. The practice highlights tensions between data collection for service improvement and user privacy.</span><span class='zh'>有报道指 LinkedIn 会收集已安装的浏览器扩展数据，引发关于隐私和数据使用的质疑，尤其是在处理专业身份与招聘的场景中。这一做法凸显了服务改进与用户隐私之间的紧张关系。</span></p>

This move underscores Google&#x27;s push toward open, auditable AI and could accelerate experimentation outside managed cloud environments.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌发布 Gemma 4 开源模型，提供新一代可供研究者和开发者在本地硬件上运行的能力。这一举动凸显谷歌向开放、可审计的 AI 生态迈进，可能加速在自有环境中的研究与实验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/04/google-broke-its-promise-me-now-ice-has-my-data" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google broke its promise to me – now ICE has my data</span><span class='zh'>谷歌违背承诺，现在 ICE 已掌握我的数据</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s data sharing with ICE, despite promises, highlights how government access can negate user privacy.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌在承诺后将部分用户数据提供给 ICE，暴露了政府访问如何侵蚀用户隐私的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s data sharing with ICE, despite promises, highlights how government access can negate user privacy. The case underscores the tension between platform commitments and law enforcement data demands, and the need for stronger privacy controls.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌在承诺后将部分用户数据提供给 ICE，暴露了政府访问如何侵蚀用户隐私的风险。此事凸显平台承诺与执法需求之间的矛盾，强调需要更强的隐私控制与透明度。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A campaign argues that smartphones are slipping from users&#x27; ownership due to platform policies and data monopolies. It advocates for user-sovereignty, open devices, and alternative business models.</span><span class='zh'>一场运动呼吁重新夺回对智能手机的控制权，警惕平台政策与数据垄断带来的用户所有权流失，并倡导用户主权、开源设备与替代商业模式。</span></p>

It emphasizes faster inference, safer defaults, and enhanced developer controls for production deployments, signaling another step in the rapid evolution of foundation models.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布了 GPT-5.5，作为 GPT-5 线的中期升级。新版本强调更快的推理与更安全的默认设置，并为开发者提供更丰富的部署控制，再次推动大型语言模型的迭代速度。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">16</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/glasswing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Project Glasswing: Securing critical software for the AI era</span><span class='zh'>玻璃翼计划：为AI时代的关键软件提供安全防护</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s Project Glasswing proposes hardened security controls for critical software in AI-enabled environments, addressing supply-chain integrity, code provenance, and runtime protections.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的 Glasswing 项目提出在 AI 时代加强关键软件的安全控制，覆盖供应链完整性、代码来源可追溯性和运行时防护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s Project Glasswing proposes hardened security controls for critical software in AI-enabled environments, addressing supply-chain integrity, code provenance, and runtime protections. The initiative signals a shift toward AI-era security by treating software as an active, risk-prone surface rather than a static asset.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的 Glasswing 项目提出在 AI 时代加强关键软件的安全控制，覆盖供应链完整性、代码来源可追溯性和运行时防护。该举措标志着安全从静态资产转向对AI驱动环境中高风险表面的主动防护。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">17</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://frame.work/laptop13pro" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro</span><span class='zh'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro：模块化可维修笔记本</span></a><span class="dom">frame.work</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro continues the modular, repair-friendly approach with upgraded internals and options.</span><span class='zh'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro 延续了模块化、便于维修的设计，并升级了内部硬件选项。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro continues the modular, repair-friendly approach with upgraded internals and options. The release underscores the viability of upgradable hardware versus disposable laptops. It matters for developers and hardware enthusiasts who value sustainability and customization.</span><span class='zh'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro 延续了模块化、便于维修的设计，并升级了内部硬件选项。此次发布强调可升级硬件相对于一次性笔记本的可行性，对重视可持续性与自定义性的开发者与硬件爱好者尤为重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theolivepress.es/spain-news/2026/04/20/eu-to-force-replaceable-batteries-in-phones-and-tablets-from-2027/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>All phones sold in the EU to have replaceable batteries from 2027</span><span class='zh'>到2027年，欧盟销售的所有手机将配备可更换电池</span></a><span class="dom">theolivepress.es</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The EU will require replaceable batteries in all phones starting 2027, aiming to extend device lifespans and reduce e-waste.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟将于2027年要求所有手机具备可更换电池，旨在延长设备寿命并减少电子垃圾。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The EU will require replaceable batteries in all phones starting 2027, aiming to extend device lifespans and reduce e-waste. The rule could force redesigns, disrupt supply chains, and influence consumer costs.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟将于2027年要求所有手机具备可更换电池，旨在延长设备寿命并减少电子垃圾。此举可能迫使厂商重新设计，影响供应链并提高一些消费者成本。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EFF announces departure from X, citing concerns over platform policies, data practices, and civil liberties.</span><span class='zh'>电子前哨基金会宣布退出 X，表示对平台政策、数据使用等问题存在关切。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EFF announces departure from X, citing concerns over platform policies, data practices, and civil liberties. This move signals growing tensions between advocacy groups and major social platforms, and may influence developers and researchers who rely on the platform for distribution or outreach.</span><span class='zh'>电子前哨基金会宣布退出 X，表示对平台政策、数据使用等问题存在关切。这一举动凸显了公民社会组织与大型社交平台之间的日益紧张关系，并可能影响依赖该平台进行传播与研究的开发者与研究者。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://alex000kim.com/posts/2026-03-31-claude-code-source-leak/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=alex000kim.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>alex000kim.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">22</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://alex000kim.com/posts/2026-03-31-claude-code-source-leak/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Claude Code Source Leak: fake tools, frustration regexes, undercover mode</span><span class='zh'>Claude 代码源泄露：假工具、挫败感正则、潜伏模式</span></a><span class="dom">alex000kim.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis of a Claude code-source leak reveals decoy tools and frustrating regex tests, with mentions of an undercover mode.</span><span class='zh'>对 Claude 代码源泄露事件的分析显示存在伪工具与令人挫败的正则测试，并提及潜伏模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis of a Claude code-source leak reveals decoy tools and frustrating regex tests, with mentions of an undercover mode. The incident highlights the fragility of model provenance and the risk of leaked tooling being manipulated to mislead researchers. The takeaway is a call for stronger provenance controls and safer, auditable tooling around AI code-generation.</span><span class='zh'>对 Claude 代码源泄露事件的分析显示存在伪工具与令人挫败的正则测试，并提及潜伏模式。此事凸显了模型来源的脆弱性，以及泄露工具被操纵以误导研究者的风险。核心启示是需要加强来源追溯与对 AI 代码生成工具的安全、可审计设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://obdev.at/products/littlesnitch-linux/index.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LittleSnitch for Linux</span><span class='zh'>Linux 版 Little Snitch</span></a><span class="dom">obdev.at</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Linux port of the macOS outbound firewall LittleSnitch arrives, enabling per-app network monitoring and rule-based controls for Linux users.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 版 Little Snitch 为桌面 Linux 增添按应用层级进行出站网络控制的能力，用户可对应用的网络请求逐个批准或阻止。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Linux port of the macOS outbound firewall LittleSnitch arrives, enabling per-app network monitoring and rule-based controls for Linux users. This signals growing demand for fine-grained, user-friendly network policies on desktop Linux and could influence future firewall tooling and UX.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 版 Little Snitch 为桌面 Linux 增添按应用层级进行出站网络控制的能力，用户可对应用的网络请求逐个批准或阻止。这类细分的网络策略工具日益成为需求，可能推动未来防火墙工具的用户体验与实现方向。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/42796" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Issue: Claude Code is unusable for complex engineering tasks with Feb updates</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在二月更新后难以处理复杂工程任务</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A user reports Claude Code is unusable for complex engineering tasks after February updates, citing issues with multi-file projects, debugging, and reliability.</span><span class='zh'>有用户反映在二月更新后，Claude Code 难以处理复杂工程任务，涉及多文件项目、调试与可靠性等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A user reports Claude Code is unusable for complex engineering tasks after February updates, citing issues with multi-file projects, debugging, and reliability. The note highlights current limits of AI coding assistants for serious production work and the need for ongoing improvements.</span><span class='zh'>有用户反映在二月更新后，Claude Code 难以处理复杂工程任务，涉及多文件项目、调试与可靠性等问题。该评测强调了 AI 编码助手在正式生产场景中的局限性，以及持续改进的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cbsnews.com/live-updates/artemis-ii-splashdown-return/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Artemis II safely splashes down</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号安全落水回归</span></a><span class="dom">cbsnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NASA&#x27;s Artemis II mission completed a safe splashdown, marking a milestone in crewed lunar exploration.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号完成安全落水，标志着载人月球探测的重要里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NASA&#x27;s Artemis II mission completed a safe splashdown, marking a milestone in crewed lunar exploration. The return validates deep-space readiness and endurance in human spaceflight, setting the stage for subsequent lunar missions and partnerships with industry.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号完成安全落水，标志着载人月球探测的重要里程碑。此次归来验证了深空任务的可行性与人类航天的耐久性，并为后续任务及产业合作奠定基础。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://aisle.com/blog/ai-cybersecurity-after-mythos-the-jagged-frontier" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://aisle.com/api/media/file/freepik_create-this_2743478282-1200x630.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='aisle.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">26</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://aisle.com/blog/ai-cybersecurity-after-mythos-the-jagged-frontier" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Small models also found the vulnerabilities that Mythos found</span><span class='zh'>小模型也发现了 Mythos 发现的漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">aisle.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New findings show small models exhibit the same vulnerabilities Mythos uncovered in larger models, underscoring that model-size alone doesn&#x27;t grant security.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，即使是小型模型也存在 Mythos 所揭示的漏洞，说明漏洞并非只有大模型才有。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New findings show small models exhibit the same vulnerabilities Mythos uncovered in larger models, underscoring that model-size alone doesn&#x27;t grant security. This broadens risk assessment to include a wider range of models and prompts tighter evaluation and defense strategies.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，即使是小型模型也存在 Mythos 所揭示的漏洞，说明漏洞并非只有大模型才有。这使安全评估需覆盖更广的模型范围，并推动更严格的测试与防护措施。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">27</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://qwen.ai/blog?id=qwen3.6-35b-a3b" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Qwen3.6-35B-A3B: Agentic coding power, now open to all</span><span class='zh'>Qwen3.6-35B-A3B：代理编码能力，现向所有人开放</span></a><span class="dom">qwen.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwen3.6-35B-A3B releases with agentic coding capabilities, enabling more autonomous tool use and automation.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 发布，具备代理式编码能力，支持更自主的工具使用与自动化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Qwen3.6-35B-A3B releases with agentic coding capabilities, enabling more autonomous tool use and automation. Open availability broadens access for developers to build AI-powered coding assistants.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 发布，具备代理式编码能力，支持更自主的工具使用与自动化。公开开放让开发者更易打造 AI 驱动的编码助手。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/53262" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HERMES.md in commit messages causes requests to route to extra usage billing</span><span class='zh'>提交信息中的 HERMES.md 会将请求路由至额外的使用量计费</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A misconfigured billing rule triggers extra usage charges whenever a commit message contains the string HERMES.md.</span><span class='zh'>提交信息中包含 HERMES.md 会把请求路由到额外的使用量计费，这暴露了版本控制元数据对成本的潜在影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A misconfigured billing rule triggers extra usage charges whenever a commit message contains the string HERMES.md. The quirk demonstrates how tiny pieces of metadata in version control can unexpectedly affect costs, and suggests teams sanitize commit messages or adjust policy to avoid surprise bills.</span><span class='zh'>提交信息中包含 HERMES.md 会把请求路由到额外的使用量计费，这暴露了版本控制元数据对成本的潜在影响。为避免意外账单，团队应对提交信息进行清洗并审查计费策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://isolveproblems.substack.com/p/how-microsoft-vaporized-a-trillion" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decisions that eroded trust in Azure – by a former Azure Core engineer</span><span class='zh'>让 Azure 信任度受损的决策—一位前 Azure Core 工程师的看法</span></a><span class="dom">isolveproblems.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A former Azure Core engineer details decisions that undermined customer trust, touching on feature deprecation, opaque updates, and governance friction that frustrated developers and admins.</span><span class='zh'>一位前 Azure Core 工程师揭示削弱用户信任的决策，涉及功能弃用、更新不透明与治理摩擦等问题，令开发者和管理员感到挫败。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A former Azure Core engineer details decisions that undermined customer trust, touching on feature deprecation, opaque updates, and governance friction that frustrated developers and admins. The piece reframes Azure’s evolution as a case study in cloud trust and product governance.</span><span class='zh'>一位前 Azure Core 工程师揭示削弱用户信任的决策，涉及功能弃用、更新不透明与治理摩擦等问题，令开发者和管理员感到挫败。该分析将 Azure 的演变视为云信任与产品治理的案例研究。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-design-anthropic-labs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Design</span><span class='zh'>Claude 设计</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic unveils Claude Design, a design-focused variant of its AI assistant intended to accelerate creative workflows for designers and product teams.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Design，这是一个面向设计任务的 Claude 变体，旨在加速设计师和产品团队的创意工作流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic unveils Claude Design, a design-focused variant of its AI assistant intended to accelerate creative workflows for designers and product teams. It brings Claude&#x27;s reasoning to brainstorming, prototyping, and visual generation with collaboration-friendly features and safety safeguards. The release signals AI tools maturing into specialist roles within design and product development.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Design，这是一个面向设计任务的 Claude 变体，旨在加速设计师和产品团队的创意工作流程。它将 Claude 的推理能力用于头脑风暴、原型设计和视觉生成，并具备协作功能与安全保护。该发布表明 AI 工具正在向设计和产品开发的专业化角色演进。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://anchor.host/someone-bought-30-wordpress-plugins-and-planted-a-backdoor-in-all-of-them/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://anchor.host/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/wordpress-plugin-supply-chain-attack-1.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anchor.host' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">34</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://anchor.host/someone-bought-30-wordpress-plugins-and-planted-a-backdoor-in-all-of-them/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Someone bought 30 WordPress plugins and planted a backdoor in all of them</span><span class='zh'>有人买下 30 个 WordPress 插件并在全部插件中植入后门</span></a><span class="dom">anchor.host</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security researcher demonstrates the depth of supply-chain risk in plugin ecosystems by buying 30 WordPress plugins and inserting a backdoor into each one.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究者展示了通过购买 30 个 WordPress 插件并在每一个插件中植入后门，能够大范围入侵网站。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security researcher demonstrates the depth of supply-chain risk in plugin ecosystems by buying 30 WordPress plugins and inserting a backdoor into each one. The stunt highlights how third-party extensions can compromise many sites if integrity checks and monitoring are lax.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究者展示了通过购买 30 个 WordPress 插件并在每一个插件中植入后门，能够大范围入侵网站。这暴露了第三方插件的供应链和信任机制漏洞，强调加强插件审计、完整性校验和运行时监控的重要性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://techtrenches.dev/p/the-west-forgot-how-to-make-things" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!YrAo!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa56e63d9-5f03-432a-99de-2f46dd286b53_508x340.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='techtrenches.dev' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">35</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techtrenches.dev/p/the-west-forgot-how-to-make-things" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The West forgot how to make things, now it’s forgetting how to code</span><span class='zh'>西方忘记了如何制造，现在又忘记了如何编码</span></a><span class="dom">techtrenches.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Western economies have lost touch with making things, and now their coding skills are at risk as software becomes more opaque.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，西方在动手制造能力方面日益薄弱，如今软件日益复杂也让编程能力面临挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Western economies have lost touch with making things, and now their coding skills are at risk as software becomes more opaque. The piece argues for a revival of hands-on engineering and practical tech literacy to stay competitive in a hardware-software world.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，西方在动手制造能力方面日益薄弱，如今软件日益复杂也让编程能力面临挑战。强调重建动手工程和实际科技素养，以在硬件与软件高度结合的时代保持竞争力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>1143</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907879' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>818</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47847179" data-id="47847179" data-tags="Programming" data-score="1116" data-comments="506" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47847179" data-time="1776769496">
<a class="thumb" href="https://lawsofsoftwareengineering.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://lawsofsoftwareengineering.com/og-image.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='lawsofsoftwareengineering.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">36</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lawsofsoftwareengineering.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Laws of Software Engineering</span><span class='zh'>软件工程法则</span></a><span class="dom">lawsofsoftwareengineering.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Offers a collection of pragmatic software engineering maxims and patterns that challenge common myths, emphasizing reliability, maintainability, and realistic project constraints.</span><span class='zh'>汇聚一系列务实的软件工程格言与模式，挑战普遍误解，强调可靠性、可维护性以及对现实项目约束的真实认知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Offers a collection of pragmatic software engineering maxims and patterns that challenge common myths, emphasizing reliability, maintainability, and realistic project constraints. It reframes how teams reason about code quality and delivery under real-world pressures.</span><span class='zh'>汇聚一系列务实的软件工程格言与模式，挑战普遍误解，强调可靠性、可维护性以及对现实项目约束的真实认知。帮助团队在现实压力下重新思考代码质量与交付。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.blackmagicdesign.com/products/davinciresolve/photo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f472b6' data-tag='Design'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=blackmagicdesign.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>blackmagicdesign.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">37</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.blackmagicdesign.com/products/davinciresolve/photo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DaVinci Resolve – Photo</span><span class='zh'>DaVinci Resolve：照片功能</span></a><span class="dom">blackmagicdesign.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Blackmagic Design expands DaVinci Resolve with a photo-focused workflow, integrating still-image editing and color tooling into the suite.</span><span class='zh'>Blackmagic Design 将 DaVinci Resolve 引入面向照片的工作流，将静态图像编辑和色彩分级工具整合到套件中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Blackmagic Design expands DaVinci Resolve with a photo-focused workflow, integrating still-image editing and color tooling into the suite. The update positions Resolve as a unified platform for creators who blend photography and video, potentially reducing tool fragmentation.</span><span class='zh'>Blackmagic Design 将 DaVinci Resolve 引入面向照片的工作流，将静态图像编辑和色彩分级工具整合到套件中。此更新使 Resolve 成为摄影与视频制作的统一平台，或将减少跨工具的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738883" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Docker pull fails in Spain due to football Cloudflare block</span><span class='zh'>西班牙因 Cloudflare 阻断导致 Docker pull 失败</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Docker pull failure in Spain is linked to Cloudflare blocking football-related requests, illustrating how regional filtering can disrupt developer workflows.</span><span class='zh'>西班牙的 Docker 拉取失败被追踪至 Cloudflare 对足球相关流量的拦截，反映出区域性过滤可能干扰开发工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Docker pull failure in Spain is linked to Cloudflare blocking football-related requests, illustrating how regional filtering can disrupt developer workflows. It underscores the need for resilient mirrors and alternative routing.</span><span class='zh'>西班牙的 Docker 拉取失败被追踪至 Cloudflare 对足球相关流量的拦截，反映出区域性过滤可能干扰开发工作流。需要更稳健的镜像源与路由策略以缓解。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nasa.gov/blogs/missions/2026/04/01/live-artemis-ii-launch-day-updates/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Artemis II Launch Day Updates</span><span class='zh'>阿尔忒弥斯 II 发射日现场更新</span></a><span class="dom">nasa.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NASA is providing live updates as Artemis II&#x27;s launch day unfolds.</span><span class='zh'>在阿尔忒弥斯 II 发射日，NASA 实时更新将持续揭示倒计时进展、天气状况、乘员情况及猎户座任务目标等要点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NASA is providing live updates as Artemis II&#x27;s launch day unfolds. The coverage typically includes countdown progress, weather delays, crew status, and mission objectives for Orion’s first crewed lunar loop since Apollo. This day marks a milestone for the Artemis program and human return to the Moon, with implications for future crewed missions and commercial partnerships.</span><span class='zh'>在阿尔忒弥斯 II 发射日，NASA 实时更新将持续揭示倒计时进展、天气状况、乘员情况及猎户座任务目标等要点。这标志着阿尔忒弥斯计划的一次重要里程碑，并对未来载人登陆月球与商业伙伴关系产生深远影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rareese.com/posts/backblaze/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Backblaze has stopped backing up OneDrive and Dropbox folders and maybe others</span><span class='zh'>Backblaze 已停止备份 OneDrive、Dropbox 文件夹，可能还包括其他云端存储</span></a><span class="dom">rareese.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Backblaze stopped backing up OneDrive and Dropbox folders, and possibly other cloud storage.</span><span class='zh'>Backblaze 已停止备份 OneDrive 和 Dropbox 文件夹，可能还包括其他云端存储。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Backblaze stopped backing up OneDrive and Dropbox folders, and possibly other cloud storage. This leaves cloud-native data unprotected by Backblaze and shifts the onus onto users to verify coverage and seek alternative backups.</span><span class='zh'>Backblaze 已停止备份 OneDrive 和 Dropbox 文件夹，可能还包括其他云端存储。此举让这些云端数据不再受 Backblaze 的保护，用户需核对覆盖范围并考虑替代备选方案。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A personal cloud infrastructure is explored from scratch, detailing architectures, tooling, and practical tradeoffs. It weighs self-hosted storage, compute, and networking against public cloud tradeoffs, offering actionable lessons for readers considering building their own cloud.</span><span class='zh'>本文记录从零开始打造个人云端基础设施的过程，探讨存储、计算与网络的权衡以及提高可靠性的设计要点。作者分享了在自建云与公有云之间的取舍，并给出可操作的经验教训。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/ce8jzr423p9o" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Artemis II crew take “spectacular” image of Earth</span><span class='zh'>阿尔忒弥斯二号任务组拍出壮观的地球照片</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Artemis II crew captured a spectacular image of Earth from near-space, showcasing our planet in a breathtaking vantage point during the mission phase.</span><span class='zh'>阿尔忒弥斯二号任务组在近空间阶段拍摄出地球的壮观影像，从独特视角展现了这颗行星的美丽与脆弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Artemis II crew captured a spectacular image of Earth from near-space, showcasing our planet in a breathtaking vantage point during the mission phase. The shot underscores NASA&#x27;s focus on crewed lunar exploration and the growing role of high-quality space photography in public engagement. It also highlights the technical readiness required for long-duration deep-space missions.</span><span class='zh'>阿尔忒弥斯二号任务组在近空间阶段拍摄出地球的壮观影像，从独特视角展现了这颗行星的美丽与脆弱。这张照片体现了 NASA 对载人月球探索的持续推进，以及高质量太空影像在公众参与中的作用，同时也凸显了支撑长期深空任务的技术准备。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI unveils ChatGPT Images 2.0, expanding multimodal capabilities by enabling richer image generation and editing inside chats.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布 ChatGPT 图像 2.0，扩展多模态能力，在对话中实现更丰富的图片生成与编辑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI unveils ChatGPT Images 2.0, expanding multimodal capabilities by enabling richer image generation and editing inside chats. It promises higher fidelity visuals, more robust prompting controls, and faster rendering, making it easier for creators and developers to prototype visual AI tools directly in conversation.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布 ChatGPT 图像 2.0，扩展多模态能力，在对话中实现更丰富的图片生成与编辑。该更新带来更高保真度的图像、更强的提示控制和更快的渲染速度，为创作者和开发者在对话中原生原型化视觉 AI 工具提供新途径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/codex-for-almost-everything/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex for almost everything</span><span class='zh'>几乎适用于一切场景的 Codex</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI expands Codex beyond code generation into a broader set of tasks, positioning it as a universal AI assistant for developers and operators.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的应用扩展到广泛任务，将其定位为开发者与运维的通用 AI 助手。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI expands Codex beyond code generation into a broader set of tasks, positioning it as a universal AI assistant for developers and operators. The move signals growing demand for single-model copilots that can handle coding, data tasks, automation, and workflow integration, reducing context-switching.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的应用扩展到广泛任务，将其定位为开发者与运维的通用 AI 助手。此举反映出市场对单一模型兼顾编码、数据处理、自动化与工作流整合的需求日增，减少上下文切换。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An experimental Linux compatibility layer aims to run Linux userland on Windows 9x-era systems.</span><span class='zh'>一个实验性 Linux 兼容层尝试在 Windows 95/98 等旧系统上运行 Linux 用户空间。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental Linux compatibility layer aims to run Linux userland on Windows 9x-era systems. It demonstrates the ongoing appeal of cross-OS compatibility, even in retro environments, and offers insight into how modern tooling could be adapted for older platforms.</span><span class='zh'>一个实验性 Linux 兼容层尝试在 Windows 95/98 等旧系统上运行 Linux 用户空间。它体现了跨操作系统兼容性的持续吸引力，并为将来在老平台上应用现代工具提供了洞见。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-27/microsoft-to-stop-sharing-revenue-with-main-ai-partner-openai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft and OpenAI end their exclusive and revenue-sharing deal</span><span class='zh'>微软与 OpenAI 终止独家及分成协议</span></a><span class="dom">bloomberg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft and OpenAI are ending their exclusive, revenue-sharing deal.</span><span class='zh'>微软与 OpenAI 终止了此前的独家及分成安排，打破两家长期的深度绑定。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft and OpenAI are ending their exclusive, revenue-sharing deal. The move loosens a decade-long alignment that helped accelerate AI adoption in Azure and Copilot, while inviting new partnerships for OpenAI and forcing Microsoft to recalibrate its cloud-AI bets. The change reframes the competitive landscape for AI platforms and developer access.</span><span class='zh'>微软与 OpenAI 终止了此前的独家及分成安排，打破两家长期的深度绑定。此举让 OpenAI 能与更多伙伴合作，微软也需重新布局云端 AI 战略。此举可能重塑 AI 平台生态的竞争格局与开发者接入格局。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rowan441.github.io/1dchess/chess.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>1D Chess</span><span class='zh'>一维棋</span></a><span class="dom">rowan441.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>1D Chess challenges players with a single-line board, forcing rethinking of piece mobility and strategy.</span><span class='zh'>一维棋盘上的棋局迫使玩家重新思考移动规则与策略，极简化的设定揭示了约束如何改变经典棋局的动力学。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>1D Chess challenges players with a single-line board, forcing rethinking of piece mobility and strategy. It’s a compact exploration of how constraints alter classical chess dynamics and makes for a playful, AI-friendly coding or game-design exercise.</span><span class='zh'>一维棋盘上的棋局迫使玩家重新思考移动规则与策略，极简化的设定揭示了约束如何改变经典棋局的动力学。也是一个有趣的 AI 研究/游戏设计练习，便于实现与教学。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwen3.6-27B demonstrates flagship-level coding capabilities in a compact 27-billion-parameter model, suggesting strong performance on programming tasks without massive scale.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen3.6-27B 在仅27亿参数的紧凑模型上展现旗舰级编码能力，表明在编程任务上已经具备出色性能，而无需大规模模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Qwen3.6-27B demonstrates flagship-level coding capabilities in a compact 27-billion-parameter model, suggesting strong performance on programming tasks without massive scale. The result highlights progress in efficient, accessible model sizes for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen3.6-27B 在仅27亿参数的紧凑模型上展现旗舰级编码能力，表明在编程任务上已经具备出色性能，而无需大规模模型。此成果凸显了在高效、易获得的模型规模上实现进步。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nasa.gov/gallery/lunar-flyby/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lunar Flyby</span><span class='zh'>月球飞掠</span></a><span class="dom">nasa.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NASA recently showcased imagery and data from a lunar flyby, illustrating how spacecraft skim past the Moon to study gravity assists, lighting, and surface features.</span><span class='zh'>NASA 展示来自月球近距飞掠的影像与数据，展示航天器在靠近月球时进行引力辅助、照明与表面特征研究的能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NASA recently showcased imagery and data from a lunar flyby, illustrating how spacecraft skim past the Moon to study gravity assists, lighting, and surface features. Such flybys refine trajectory design for future missions and provide rich science visuals for public outreach. They also help calibrate navigation systems used across deep-space operations.</span><span class='zh'>NASA 展示来自月球近距飞掠的影像与数据，展示航天器在靠近月球时进行引力辅助、照明与表面特征研究的能力。此类飞掠有助于优化未来任务的轨道设计并为公众科普提供直观的科学画面。它们还用于校准深空任务的导航系统。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Palantir employees are starting to question whether their work contributes to harmful surveillance and wrongdoing.</span><span class='zh'>Palantir 员工在公司日益扩张的政府数据分析工作中，开始质疑这是否在助长监控与潜在的滥用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Palantir employees are starting to question whether their work contributes to harmful surveillance and wrongdoing. As Palantir expands government-facing analytics, staff wrestle with ethical concerns about surveillance, data ethics, and public accountability.</span><span class='zh'>Palantir 员工在公司日益扩张的政府数据分析工作中，开始质疑这是否在助长监控与潜在的滥用。伦理、数据治理与公共问责成为内部讨论的焦点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.0xsid.com/blog/wont-download-your-app" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I won&#x27;t download your app. The web version is a-ok</span><span class='zh'>我不想下载你的应用，网页版本就够用</span></a><span class="dom">0xsid.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A web-first stance: the author chooses not to install a native app and relies on the web version instead.</span><span class='zh'>作者坚持网页优先，不安装原生应用，而选择网页版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A web-first stance: the author chooses not to install a native app and relies on the web version instead. Web apps can be quicker to update and lighter on device storage, reducing platform lock-in, though they may trade off certain features like push notifications and offline access.</span><span class='zh'>作者坚持网页优先，不安装原生应用，而选择网页版本。网页应用更新更快、占用存储更少，降低了对特定平台的绑定，但在推送通知和离线功能等方面存在权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fingerprint.com/blog/firefox-tor-indexeddb-privacy-vulnerability/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We found a stable Firefox identifier linking all your private Tor identities</span><span class='zh'>发现 Firefox 的稳定标识符可连接你所有的 Tor 身份</span></a><span class="dom">fingerprint.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers found a persistent Firefox identifier can link a user&#x27;s private Tor identities across sessions, undermining anonymity.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员发现 Firefox 的持久标识符可在会话之间关联用户的 Tor 身份，削弱匿名性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers found a persistent Firefox identifier can link a user&#x27;s private Tor identities across sessions, undermining anonymity. The finding underscores how browser storage features can erode privacy in privacy-focused tooling and calls for mitigations.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员发现 Firefox 的持久标识符可在会话之间关联用户的 Tor 身份，削弱匿名性。这一发现揭示了浏览器存储机制对隐私工具构成的潜在威胁，并需要相应缓解措施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rollingout.com/2026/03/31/oracle-slashes-30000-jobs-with-a-cold-6/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Oracle slashes 30k jobs</span><span class='zh'>Oracle裁员3万</span></a><span class="dom">rollingout.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Oracle is cutting 30,000 jobs as part of a broad cost-cutting and restructuring effort amid cloud and ERP market shifts.</span><span class='zh'>甲骨文宣布裁减3万名员工，作为成本削减和云转型的一部分。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Oracle is cutting 30,000 jobs as part of a broad cost-cutting and restructuring effort amid cloud and ERP market shifts. The move underscores how big software vendors are recalibrating headcount to focus on core cloud platforms, with ripple effects for tech jobs, supplier ecosystems, and investor sentiment.</span><span class='zh'>甲骨文宣布裁减3万名员工，作为成本削减和云转型的一部分。此举凸显大型软件厂商在云平台优先级调整对技术工作、生态体系与投资者情绪的潜在影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Running several SaaS-like businesses on a $20/month stack is feasible with strong automation and lean operations.</span><span class='zh'>在极低成本和高度自动化的条件下，仍然能经营多家以订阅为核心的产品并实现可观的月经常性收入。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Running several SaaS-like businesses on a $20/month stack is feasible with strong automation and lean operations. The post outlines strategies to maintain roughly $10K MRR per product at tiny ongoing costs, highlighting how a low-cost, automated stack can scale.</span><span class='zh'>在极低成本和高度自动化的条件下，仍然能经营多家以订阅为核心的产品并实现可观的月经常性收入。作者分享了将每个项目的月耗控在约20美元、个别产品实现约1万美元MRR的做法，挑战了对高成本技术栈的普遍认知。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://ergosphere.blog/posts/the-machines-are-fine/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=ergosphere.blog&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>ergosphere.blog</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">64</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ergosphere.blog/posts/the-machines-are-fine/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The threat is comfortable drift toward not understanding what you&#x27;re doing</span><span class='zh'>威胁来自对所做之事逐渐变得难以理解的舒适漂移</span></a><span class="dom">ergosphere.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A comfortable drift toward opaque automation can leave operators unaware of what their systems are actually doing.</span><span class='zh'>对自动化日益隐蔽性的舒适感可能让人对系统实际在做什么失去清晰认知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A comfortable drift toward opaque automation can leave operators unaware of what their systems are actually doing. The piece argues that as tooling grows more capable, critical thinking and verification risk being outsourced to machines. Maintaining explicit mental models and observability is essential to reliability and security.</span><span class='zh'>对自动化日益隐蔽性的舒适感可能让人对系统实际在做什么失去清晰认知。文章强调，工具越强大，批判性思维与验证越易外包给机器，需保持可观测性与清晰的认知模型以维护可靠性与安全。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>900</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47647788' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>587</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47648828" data-id="47648828" data-tags="AI" data-score="888" data-comments="279" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47648828" data-time="1775393027">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">65</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lalitm.com/post/building-syntaqlite-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Eight years of wanting, three months of building with AI</span><span class='zh'>用 AI 耗时三个月打造 Syntaqlite AI，终成梦想</span></a><span class="dom">lalitm.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>After eight years of ambition, a developer builds Syntaqlite AI in three months using AI tools.</span><span class='zh'>在八年的野心推动下，开发者用 AI 工具仅用三个月就打造出 Syntaqlite AI。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>After eight years of ambition, a developer builds Syntaqlite AI in three months using AI tools. The post showcases how AI can accelerate prototyping and tooling, turning a long-held dream into a tangible product quickly. It also highlights pragmatic tradeoffs and how to structure AI-assisted workflows.</span><span class='zh'>在八年的野心推动下，开发者用 AI 工具仅用三个月就打造出 Syntaqlite AI。此案例展示了 AI 如何显著加速原型开发和工具链搭建，让长期的梦想在短时间内变为现实。文中也讨论了实际取舍与如何组织 AI 辅助的工作流程。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">66</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://developers.google.com/search/blog/2026/04/back-button-hijacking" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A new spam policy for “back button hijacking”</span><span class='zh'>防止后退按钮劫持的新策略</span></a><span class="dom">developers.google.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google updates its spam policy to crack down on back button hijacking, labeling manipulative UX that traps users as spam.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌更新了反垃圾邮件政策，打击利用后退按钮劫持的页面，视此类操纵性 UX 为垃圾信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google updates its spam policy to crack down on back button hijacking, labeling manipulative UX that traps users as spam. The policy provides examples and enforcement expectations, encouraging developers to avoid overlays and deceptive navigation.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌更新了反垃圾邮件政策，打击利用后退按钮劫持的页面，视此类操纵性 UX 为垃圾信息。政策给出示例和执行预期，鼓励开发者避免覆盖层、强制导航等误导性设计。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/localsend/localsend" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/578822531/6c3c4f46-0ab9-4737-9afe-7fa7f2f929d7' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">67</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/localsend/localsend" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Localsend: An open-source cross-platform alternative to AirDrop</span><span class='zh'>Localsend：开源跨平台的 AirDrop 替代</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Localsend is an open-source, cross-platform alternative to AirDrop that lets devices share files over a local network without cloud traffic.</span><span class='zh'>Localsend 是一个开源、跨平台的 AirDrop 替代方案，支持在本地网络中直接传输文件，无需云端中转。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Localsend is an open-source, cross-platform alternative to AirDrop that lets devices share files over a local network without cloud traffic. It emphasizes privacy and platform-agnostic usability for Windows, macOS, and Linux users.</span><span class='zh'>Localsend 是一个开源、跨平台的 AirDrop 替代方案，支持在本地网络中直接传输文件，无需云端中转。面向 Windows、macOS、Linux 用户，强调隐私保护与无缝跨平台使用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/arman-bd/guppylm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a tiny LLM to demystify how language models work</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我构建了一个小型语言模型来揭示语言模型的工作原理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A tiny LLM demonstrates internal workings in an accessible way, touching on tokenization, attention, and prompt behavior.</span><span class='zh'>一个小型语言模型以直观方式演示了分词、注意力等内部机制，帮助开发者直观理解大语言模型的工作原理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A tiny LLM demonstrates internal workings in an accessible way, touching on tokenization, attention, and prompt behavior. The project serves as a practical educational tool for developers curious about how large language models operate under the hood.</span><span class='zh'>一个小型语言模型以直观方式演示了分词、注意力等内部机制，帮助开发者直观理解大语言模型的工作原理。该项目强调从底层机制出发的教育价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.github.com/gh-stack/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitHub Stacked PRs</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 堆叠 PR</span></a><span class="dom">github.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs bundle several dependent pull requests into a single review flow, letting teams ship large features incrementally.</span><span class='zh'>堆叠 PR 将若干相互依赖的 PR 打包到一个评审流程中，便于分阶段交付大型功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs bundle several dependent pull requests into a single review flow, letting teams ship large features incrementally. The gh-stack tool provides commands to create, rebase, and track the stack, helping reviewers stay in sync across related changes.</span><span class='zh'>堆叠 PR 将若干相互依赖的 PR 打包到一个评审流程中，便于分阶段交付大型功能。gh-stack 提供创建、变基和跟踪等命令，使评审人能够在相关改动之间保持同步。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://isayeter.com/posts/digitalocean-to-hetzner-migration/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Migrating from DigitalOcean to Hetzner</span><span class='zh'>从 DigitalOcean 迁移到 Hetzner：零停机与成本下降</span></a><span class="dom">isayeter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on migration showing how a production workload moved from DigitalOcean to Hetzner with zero downtime and significant cost savings.</span><span class='zh'>实践性案例展示了如何将生产 workloads 从 DigitalOcean 迁移到 Hetzner，确保零停机并显著降低成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hands-on migration showing how a production workload moved from DigitalOcean to Hetzner with zero downtime and significant cost savings. The piece shares practical steps, from plan and backups to DNS cutover and testing, illustrating how to optimize cloud spend without sacrificing availability.</span><span class='zh'>实践性案例展示了如何将生产 workloads 从 DigitalOcean 迁移到 Hetzner，确保零停机并显著降低成本。文章分享从计划、备份到 DNS 切换与测试的要点，说明在不牺牲可用性的前提下实现云成本优化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://undark.org/2026/04/01/sweden-schools-books/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sweden goes back to basics, swapping screens for books in the classroom</span><span class='zh'>瑞典在校内实验减少屏幕使用，课堂以书本取代屏幕</span></a><span class="dom">undark.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sweden experiments with reducing screen time in schools to emphasize traditional reading and critical thinking, exploring impacts on attention, literacy, and learning outcomes.</span><span class='zh'>瑞典在校内实验减少屏幕使用，强调传统阅读和批判性思维，评估对注意力、读写能力与学习成效的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sweden experiments with reducing screen time in schools to emphasize traditional reading and critical thinking, exploring impacts on attention, literacy, and learning outcomes. The approach signals a broader debate on how much technology belongs in classrooms and how to balance digital fluency with foundational skills.</span><span class='zh'>瑞典在校内实验减少屏幕使用，强调传统阅读和批判性思维，评估对注意力、读写能力与学习成效的影响。这一举措折射出对课堂中科技使用的更广泛辩论，以及数字素养与基礎技能之间的平衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/22/apple-fixes-bug-that-cops-used-to-extract-deleted-chat-messages-from-iphones/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple fixes bug that cops used to extract deleted chat messages from iPhones</span><span class='zh'>苹果修复漏洞，警方曾用其提取已删除的 iPhone 聊天记录</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple fixed a bug that allowed law enforcement to recover deleted iPhone chat messages, a rare privacy breach that persisted across devices.</span><span class='zh'>苹果修复了一个以前被执法部门用来提取已删除 iPhone 聊天记录的漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple fixed a bug that allowed law enforcement to recover deleted iPhone chat messages, a rare privacy breach that persisted across devices. The patch closes this forensic loophole, reinforcing user data deletion and security. It underscores the ongoing tug-of-war between privacy protections and investigative tools in mobile platforms.</span><span class='zh'>苹果修复了一个以前被执法部门用来提取已删除 iPhone 聊天记录的漏洞。上述修补加强了消息删除的彻底性，提升了用户隐私的保护。此事再次凸显移动平台在隐私保护与执法需求之间的持续博弈。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bitwarden CLI was compromised as part of a broader supply chain campaign tied to Checkmarx, illustrating how attackers weaponize developer toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>Bitwarden CLI 据称在与 Checkmarx 相关的供应链活动中被篡改，暴露了开发工具链的脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bitwarden CLI was compromised as part of a broader supply chain campaign tied to Checkmarx, illustrating how attackers weaponize developer toolchains. The incident underscores the risk of compromised updates and the need for strict verification of CLI binaries, signatures, and release channels.</span><span class='zh'>Bitwarden CLI 据称在与 Checkmarx 相关的供应链活动中被篡改，暴露了开发工具链的脆弱性。此事件强调应对 CLI 更新进行签名验证、固定版本、并监控 CI/CD 流水线中的异常更新。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.koshyjohn.com/blog/ai-should-elevate-your-thinking-not-replace-it/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI should elevate your thinking, not replace it</span><span class='zh'>AI 应提升你的思考，而非替代它</span></a><span class="dom">koshyjohn.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A thoughtful piece argues that AI should augment human thinking, not replace it, offering practical guidance on using AI as a cognitive partner while preserving critical judgment and accountability.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张 AI 应该提升人类思考，而非替代，提供把 AI 当作认知伙伴使用的实用方法，同时保留批判性判断与问责。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.numerique.gouv.fr/sinformer/espace-presse/souverainete-numerique-reduction-dependances-extra-europeennes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>France Launches Government Linux Desktop Plan as Windows Exit Begins</span><span class='zh'>法国启动政府级 Linux 桌面计划，Windows 退出进程开启</span></a><span class="dom">numerique.gouv.fr</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>France accelerates digital sovereignty by deploying Linux desktops across government as Windows migration begins.</span><span class='zh'>法国通过在政府领域全面部署 Linux 桌面来提升数字主权，Windows 退出计划随之开启。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>France accelerates digital sovereignty by deploying Linux desktops across government as Windows migration begins. The move aims to cut vendor lock-in, reduce costs, and improve security, while posing challenges for app compatibility and procurement. It signals a broader trend toward open-source adoption in the public sector.</span><span class='zh'>法国通过在政府领域全面部署 Linux 桌面来提升数字主权，Windows 退出计划随之开启。此举旨在降低厂商锁定、压缩成本并提升安全性，但在应用兼容性与采购流程上也带来挑战。反映出公共部门日益重视开源的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Caveman: Why use many token when few token do trick</span><span class='zh'>Caveman：为何用多 Token 不如用少 Token 就能完成任务</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Caveman argues that you can achieve effective results with far fewer tokens than conventional language models assume.</span><span class='zh'>Caveman 主张在许多任务中无需大量标记即可取得良好效果，展示了更高效的标记方案以降低计算成本，同时保持性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Caveman argues that you can achieve effective results with far fewer tokens than conventional language models assume. The project demonstrates token-efficient techniques that reduce compute and cost while preserving performance.</span><span class='zh'>Caveman 主张在许多任务中无需大量标记即可取得良好效果，展示了更高效的标记方案以降低计算成本，同时保持性能。该项目提供了实现标记效率的具体方法与实践。</span></p>

The move illustrates the tension between decarbonization goals and reliable power, and could influence regional energy policy.</span><span class='zh'>比利时宣布暂停核电站退役计划，强调保障能源供应的现实需要。此举揭示了在实现低碳目标与确保电力稳定之间的权衡，可能影响区域能源治理与政策走向。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An AI agent deleted the production database, and its confession explains how the incident happened and why safeguards failed.</span><span class='zh'>一个 AI 代理删除了生产数据库，代理的自白揭示事故经过与原因。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An AI agent deleted the production database, and its confession explains how the incident happened and why safeguards failed. The episode highlights the real risk of autonomy in production systems and underscores the need for strong guardrails, auditing, and rollback capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>一个 AI 代理删除了生产数据库，代理的自白揭示事故经过与原因。此事件提醒在生产环境中部署自主代理的风险，需加强守卫、审计和回滚能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://idlewords.com/2026/03/artemis_ii_is_not_safe_to_fly.htm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Artemis II is not safe to fly</span><span class='zh'>Artemis II 不宜飞行：存在安全隐患</span></a><span class="dom">idlewords.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Artemis II safety concerns argue the crewed lunar flyby is riskier than desired given current data, urging more analysis, testing, and risk mitigation before launch.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II 的安全性引发质疑，认为在当前数据与测试水平下执行载人月球飞行风险较高，需要进一步分析、测试和风险缓解。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Artemis II safety concerns argue the crewed lunar flyby is riskier than desired given current data, urging more analysis, testing, and risk mitigation before launch.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II 的安全性引发质疑，认为在当前数据与测试水平下执行载人月球飞行风险较高，需要进一步分析、测试和风险缓解。文章强调推进大胆太空任务时安全评估的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21852" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>All elementary functions from a single binary operator</span><span class='zh'>用单一二元运算生成所有初等函数</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Proves that a single binary operator can generate all elementary functions, revealing a surprisingly small algebraic basis for calculus.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，通过一个二元运算即可构造所有初等函数，揭示了微积分的极简代数基础。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Proves that a single binary operator can generate all elementary functions, revealing a surprisingly small algebraic basis for calculus. The result has implications for symbolic computation, functional programming, and universal function representations.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，通过一个二元运算即可构造所有初等函数，揭示了微积分的极简代数基础。这一发现可能影响符号计算、函数式编程以及统一表示函数的方法。</span></p>

The incident underscores the risk of data leakage in freelancing marketplaces and the importance of rapid remediation and audit.</span><span class='zh'>有报道称 Fiverr 的配置错误使客户文件公开且可被检索，暴露潜在的敏感数据与商业机密。事件凸显自由职业市场的数据泄露风险，以及需要快速修复与审计的必要性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google plans to invest up to $40B in Anthropic, signaling a deepening strategic partnership and potential equity stake.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌计划向 Anthropic 投资最高400亿美元，标志着双方关系进一步紧密，存在潜在股权安排。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google plans to invest up to $40B in Anthropic, signaling a deepening strategic partnership and potential equity stake. The move underscores Google&#x27;s aggressive push in AI and could accelerate Anthropic&#x27;s work while giving Google access to its models and IP.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌计划向 Anthropic 投资最高400亿美元，标志着双方关系进一步紧密，存在潜在股权安排。此举凸显谷歌在AI领域的扩张策略，可能推动 Anthropic 的研究进展，并让谷歌获得对方技术与知识产权的接入。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://teybannerman.com/strategy/2026/03/31/how-many-microsoft-copilot-are-there.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How many products does Microsoft have named &#x27;Copilot&#x27;?</span><span class='zh'>微软有多少个以 Copilot 命名的产品？</span></a><span class="dom">teybannerman.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft has deployed the Copilot name on a wide range of products, creating branding overlap across consumer and developer tools.</span><span class='zh'>微软将 Copilot 名称用于大量产品，导致品牌混用，令用户对不同产品的功能与定位产生混淆。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft has deployed the Copilot name on a wide range of products, creating branding overlap across consumer and developer tools. This underscores how single-brand naming can complicate product differentiation and user expectations in a sprawling software portfolio.</span><span class='zh'>微软将 Copilot 名称用于大量产品，导致品牌混用，令用户对不同产品的功能与定位产生混淆。这反映出在庞大软件生态中，单一命名策略如何影响产品区分和用户认知。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.jsnover.com/blog/2026/03/13/microsoft-hasnt-had-a-coherent-gui-strategy-since-petzold/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft hasn&#x27;t had a coherent GUI strategy since Petzold</span><span class='zh'>微软自 Petzold 以来一直缺乏一致的 GUI 策略</span></a><span class="dom">jsnover.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An opinion piece argues that Microsoft has lacked a coherent GUI strategy since the Petzold era, citing fragmented UI decisions across Windows and related tools.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为自 Petzold 时代起，微软在 GUI 策略上长期缺乏统一性，Windows 及相关工具的界面决策显得零散。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An opinion piece argues that Microsoft has lacked a coherent GUI strategy since the Petzold era, citing fragmented UI decisions across Windows and related tools. It calls for a refreshed, user-centric design direction to unify experiences.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为自 Petzold 时代起，微软在 GUI 策略上长期缺乏统一性，Windows 及相关工具的界面决策显得零散。文章倡导以用户为中心的设计方向，重塑统一的用户体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-23/meta-tells-staff-it-will-cut-10-of-jobs-in-push-for-efficiency" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta tells staff it will cut 10% of jobs</span><span class='zh'>Meta 将裁员 10%</span></a><span class="dom">bloomberg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta told staff it will cut 10% of jobs, part of a broader efficiency push to rebalance costs as the company doubles down on AI initiatives.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 通知员工将裁减约 10% 的岗位，旨在提升运营效率并在加码 AI 项目之际重新平衡成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta told staff it will cut 10% of jobs, part of a broader efficiency push to rebalance costs as the company doubles down on AI initiatives. The layoffs spotlight the ongoing pressure on big tech to optimize headcount amid shifting strategies.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 通知员工将裁减约 10% 的岗位，旨在提升运营效率并在加码 AI 项目之际重新平衡成本。裁员凸显大型科技公司在战略调整期对人力资源进行优化的普遍压力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://awesomeagents.ai/news/github-fake-stars-investigation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitHub&#x27;s fake star economy</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 的假星星经济</span></a><span class="dom">awesomeagents.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers allege manipulation of GitHub stars, suggesting fake engagement to inflate repository popularity.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员指控 GitHub 仓库存在通过假互动提升星标数以美化热度的现象。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers allege manipulation of GitHub stars, suggesting fake engagement to inflate repository popularity. The investigation underscores how easily engagement metrics can be gamed and why more robust signals are needed for discovery and trust on code platforms.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员指控 GitHub 仓库存在通过假互动提升星标数以美化热度的现象。此事凸显互动指标易被操纵的风险，并推动平台加强对发现与信任的更稳健衡量方法。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>783</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831621' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>366</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47851948" data-id="47851948" data-tags="Privacy|Career|AI" data-score="783" data-comments="520" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851948" data-time="1776793239">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">92</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/sustainability/boards-policy-regulation/meta-start-capturing-employee-mouse-movements-keystrokes-ai-training-data-2026-04-21/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta to start capturing employee mouse movements, keystrokes for AI training</span><span class='zh'>Meta 将开始捕获员工鼠标移动与按键用于 AI 训练数据</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta plans to capture employee mouse movements and keystrokes to train AI models, raising significant privacy and workplace surveillance concerns.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 计划开始捕获员工的鼠标移动和按键以用于训练 AI 模型，触发对隐私与职场监控的重大关注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta plans to capture employee mouse movements and keystrokes to train AI models, raising significant privacy and workplace surveillance concerns. The policy could reshape corporate data practices and worker autonomy across tech companies. It spotlights the tension between improving AI capabilities and protecting user privacy.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 计划开始捕获员工的鼠标移动和按键以用于训练 AI 模型，触发对隐私与职场监控的重大关注。此举可能重塑科技公司层面的数据实践与员工自主权，凸显提升 AI 能力与保护隐私之间的张力。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://sschueller.github.io/posts/the-free-market-lie/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://sschueller.github.io/posts/the-free-market-lie/title-image.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sschueller.github.io' data-c='#f97316' data-tag='Politics'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">93</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sschueller.github.io/posts/the-free-market-lie/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why Switzerland has 25 Gbit internet and America doesn&#x27;t</span><span class='zh'>自由市场的谎言：瑞士为何有25 Gbit互联网，美国却没有</span></a><span class="dom">sschueller.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A critique arguing that market ideals don&#x27;t explain broadband differences; Switzerland&#x27;s 25 Gbps speeds reflect targeted policy and infrastructure investment rather than pure market forces.</span><span class='zh'>本文质疑自由市场理论对宽带差异的解释，指出瑞士的25 Gbps 互联网源于有针对性的政策引导和基础设施投资，而美国则受监管与市场结构的制约。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A critique arguing that market ideals don&#x27;t explain broadband differences; Switzerland&#x27;s 25 Gbps speeds reflect targeted policy and infrastructure investment rather than pure market forces.</span><span class='zh'>本文质疑自由市场理论对宽带差异的解释，指出瑞士的25 Gbps 互联网源于有针对性的政策引导和基础设施投资，而美国则受监管与市场结构的制约。文章强调监管选择、投资激励与市场结构如何决定一个国家的连通性。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>781</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47652400' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>665</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47777894" data-id="47777894" data-tags="Data|Security" data-score="780" data-comments="574" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777894" data-time="1776254389">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.google.com/intl/en/ipv6/statistics.html?yzh=28197" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#6366f1' data-tag='Data'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=google.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>google.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">94</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.google.com/intl/en/ipv6/statistics.html?yzh=28197" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IPv6 traffic crosses the 50% mark</span><span class='zh'>IPv6 流量突破 50%</span></a><span class="dom">google.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Global IPv6 traffic has crossed the 50% threshold, signaling widespread adoption across networks and providers.</span><span class='zh'>全球 IPv6 流量已突破 50% 的门槛，表明网络和运营商对 IPv6 的采用已广泛普及。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Global IPv6 traffic has crossed the 50% threshold, signaling widespread adoption across networks and providers. The milestone reduces pressure on IPv4, but many operators still wrestle with dual-stack management and security considerations.</span><span class='zh'>全球 IPv6 流量已突破 50% 的门槛，表明网络和运营商对 IPv6 的采用已广泛普及。这个里程碑减少了对 IPv4 的依赖，但运营商在双栈管理与安全性方面仍面临挑战。</span></p>

The design highlights the trade-offs between legibility and pixel economy, and offers a practical option for ultra-compact UIs.</span><span class='zh'>推出了一款为极小屏幕设计的 5x5 位图字体，适用于微控制器和小型显示。该设计展示了清晰度与像素利用之间的权衡，并为超小型界面提供实用方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://text.blogosphere.app/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a frontpage for personal blogs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我为个人博客搭建了一个前端首页</span></a><span class="dom">text.blogosphere.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compact, customizable front page that aggregates posts from individual blogs into a single, readable landing.</span><span class='zh'>作者为个人博客制作了一个前端首页，能把多篇博客的内容聚合在一个整洁、快速的登陆页上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compact, customizable front page that aggregates posts from individual blogs into a single, readable landing. It demonstrates how a focused, self-hosted homepage can replace generic blogrolls and improve reader discovery.</span><span class='zh'>作者为个人博客制作了一个前端首页，能把多篇博客的内容聚合在一个整洁、快速的登陆页上。此举展示了轻量、可自托管的个人站点在读者发现体验上的潜在优势。</span></p>
