<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">97</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/amateur-armed-with-chatgpt-vibe-maths-a-60-year-old-problem/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Amateur armed with ChatGPT solves an Erdős problem</span><span class='zh'>业余者借助 ChatGPT 解决 Erdős 问题</span></a><span class="dom">scientificamerican.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An amateur uses ChatGPT to tackle a long-standing Erdős problem and reports a result that some observers find plausible.</span><span class='zh'>一位业余爱好者借助 ChatGPT 尝试解决一个长期困扰的 Erdős 问题，称得出一个看似合理的结果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An amateur uses ChatGPT to tackle a long-standing Erdős problem and reports a result that some observers find plausible. This episode underscores AI&#x27;s potential to assist mathematical thinking while highlighting the need for rigorous verification of AI-generated proofs. It also raises questions about the boundary between exploration and formal proof.</span><span class='zh'>一位业余爱好者借助 ChatGPT 尝试解决一个长期困扰的 Erdős 问题，称得出一个看似合理的结果。此事件凸显了人工智能在辅助数学思考方面的潜力，同时也强调对 AI 生成证明进行严格验证的必要性。它还引发了关于探索与正式证明边界的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/github-copilot-is-moving-to-usage-based-billing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitHub Copilot is moving to usage-based billing</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Copilot 将改为按使用量计费</span></a><span class="dom">github.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub Copilot shifts to usage-based billing, moving away from subscription pricing; developers will pay based on usage, raising concerns about cost predictability and potential impact on adoption.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Copilot 将从订阅制转为按使用量计费，开发者需按实际使用支付费用，可能影响成本可预测性和采用程度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub Copilot shifts to usage-based billing, moving away from subscription pricing; developers will pay based on usage, raising concerns about cost predictability and potential impact on adoption. GitHub cites fairer pricing linked to value delivered.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Copilot 将从订阅制转为按使用量计费，开发者需按实际使用支付费用，可能影响成本可预测性和采用程度。GitHub 表示新定价更公平、与所创造的价值挂钩。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Multiple U.S. cities are dropping Flock Safety license-plate surveillance programs amid privacy and civil-liberties concerns.</span><span class='zh'>多座美国城市在隐私与公民自由关注下撤回Flock Safety的车牌监控项目。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Multiple U.S. cities are dropping Flock Safety license-plate surveillance programs amid privacy and civil-liberties concerns. The move underscores the tension between public safety aims and individual rights in a surveillance-heavy era.</span><span class='zh'>多座美国城市在隐私与公民自由关注下撤回Flock Safety的车牌监控项目。这一举措凸显了在高强度监控时代，公共安全目标与个人权利之间的张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://talkie-lm.com/introducing-talkie" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Talkie: a 13B vintage language model from 1930</span><span class='zh'>Talkie：一個13B參數的1930年代風格語言模型</span></a><span class="dom">talkie-lm.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Talkie introduces a 13B parameter language model built around vintage, 1930s-inspired constraints, challenging modern assumptions about scale.</span><span class='zh'>Talkie 以1930年代的設計約束為背景，打造一個13B參數的語言模型，挑戰以規模取勝的現代觀點。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Talkie introduces a 13B parameter language model built around vintage, 1930s-inspired constraints, challenging modern assumptions about scale. The project explores how era-specific design choices impact capabilities and efficiency, offering a playful, instructive counterpoint to conventional LLM development.</span><span class='zh'>Talkie 以1930年代的設計約束為背景，打造一個13B參數的語言模型，挑戰以規模取勝的現代觀點。該項目探討不同時代下的設計取捨如何影響模型能力與效率，為傳統大模型開發提供有趣且具啟發性的對照。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.phoronix.com/news/Steam-On-Linux-Tops-5p" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Steam on Linux Use Skyrocketed Above 5% in March</span><span class='zh'>Steam 在 Linux 的使用率于三月突破 5%</span></a><span class="dom">phoronix.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Steam usage on Linux climbed past 5% in March, signaling growing gamer adoption on Linux platforms.</span><span class='zh'>三月 Linux 上 Steam 用户占比超过 5%，显示 Linux 平台的玩家采纳在增加。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Steam usage on Linux climbed past 5% in March, signaling growing gamer adoption on Linux platforms. Driven by Proton/Steam Deck and better driver support, this uptick could influence game porting decisions, driver development, and Linux ecosystem viability for gaming.</span><span class='zh'>三月 Linux 上 Steam 用户占比超过 5%，显示 Linux 平台的玩家采纳在增加。受 Proton/Steam Deck 和驱动支持改善推动，这一 uptick 可能影响游戏移植决策、驱动开发以及 Linux 的游戏生态可行性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.lawfaremedia.org/article/it-is-time-to-ban-the-sale-of-precise-geolocation" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://lawfare-assets-new.azureedge.net/assets/images/default-source/article-images/world-map-cybersecurity.jpg?sfvrsn=5d9bbb64_4' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='lawfaremedia.org' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">103</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.lawfaremedia.org/article/it-is-time-to-ban-the-sale-of-precise-geolocation" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ban the sale of precise geolocation</span><span class='zh'>是时候禁止精确地理位置数据的买卖</span></a><span class="dom">lawfaremedia.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that precise geolocation data enables pervasive surveillance and targeted manipulation; advocates policy to ban its sale and transfer.</span><span class='zh'>该文章认为精确地理位置信息的买卖会带来广泛的监控与定向影响，主张禁止此类交易并提高数据中介的问责。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that precise geolocation data enables pervasive surveillance and targeted manipulation; advocates policy to ban its sale and transfer. It calls for tighter data-broker accountability, with attention to enforcement and alternatives for legitimate use cases like emergency services.</span><span class='zh'>该文章认为精确地理位置信息的买卖会带来广泛的监控与定向影响，主张禁止此类交易并提高数据中介的问责。讨论执法难点与对紧急服务等正当用途的平衡，强调需要更严格的监管框架。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>745</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806304' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>194</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47739260" data-id="47739260" data-tags="DevOps|Code" data-score="736" data-comments="648" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739260" data-time="1775999731">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pro Max 5x quota exhausted in 1.5 hours despite moderate usage</span><span class='zh'>Pro Max 5x 配额在 1.5 小时内耗尽，使用量中等</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Quotas can throttle heavy workloads far quicker than one might expect, even with moderate activity.</span><span class='zh'>在中等使用量下，配额在1.5小时内就被耗尽，暴露了按用户设定的限额在持续工作中的约束。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Quotas can throttle heavy workloads far quicker than one might expect, even with moderate activity. This case underscores the importance of understanding rate limits and having fallback or multi-account strategies for critical tasks.</span><span class='zh'>在中等使用量下，配额在1.5小时内就被耗尽，暴露了按用户设定的限额在持续工作中的约束。此事提醒开发者在规划工作流时要考虑配额上限、突发流量以及备选方案。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>736</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739260' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>648</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47804965" data-id="47804965" data-tags="Culture|Entertainment|AI" data-score="732" data-comments="288" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47804965" data-time="1776427279">
<a class="thumb" href="https://hex.ooo/library/last_question.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#d946ef' data-tag='Culture'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=hex.ooo&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>hex.ooo</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">105</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hex.ooo/library/last_question.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Isaac Asimov: The Last Question (1956)</span><span class='zh'>艾萨克·阿西莫夫：《最后的问题》（1956）</span></a><span class="dom">hex.ooo</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Asimov’s 1956 short story follows humanity&#x27;s recurring question about reversing entropy, answered by increasingly capable computer systems across epochs.</span><span class='zh'>1956 年，阿西莫夫的短篇讲述人类不断提出逆转熵的问题，由日渐强大的计算机系统在不同历史阶段给出答案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Asimov’s 1956 short story follows humanity&#x27;s recurring question about reversing entropy, answered by increasingly capable computer systems across epochs. The narrative foreshadows core AI issues—scaling capabilities, interpretability, and the cost of pursuing unbounded optimization. It remains a touchstone for long-term thinking about technology&#x27;s reach and risks.</span><span class='zh'>1956 年，阿西莫夫的短篇讲述人类不断提出逆转熵的问题，由日渐强大的计算机系统在不同历史阶段给出答案。故事预示了 AI 扩展能力、可解释性以及追求无界优化的代价等核心议题。至今，它仍是关于技术影响及长期风险思考的重要参照。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span><span class='tag' data-t='Entertainment' style='--c:#c084fc'>Entertainment</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>732</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47804965' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>288</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47792718" data-id="47792718" data-tags="Politics|Programming" data-score="720" data-comments="753" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792718" data-time="1776346333">
<a class="thumb" href="https://aphyr.com/posts/420-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess-where-do-we-go-from-here" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f97316' data-tag='Politics'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=aphyr.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>aphyr.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">106</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://aphyr.com/posts/420-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess-where-do-we-go-from-here" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The future of everything is lies, I guess: Where do we go from here?</span><span class='zh'>未来一切都是谎言？我们该往何处走？</span></a><span class="dom">aphyr.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Aphyr argues that grand narratives around &#x27;the future of everything&#x27; are dangerous and that we need pragmatic, testable approaches to technology.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为关于‘一切的未来’的宏大叙事往往误导，需要以可验证、务实的技术路径取代。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Aphyr argues that grand narratives around &#x27;the future of everything&#x27; are dangerous and that we need pragmatic, testable approaches to technology. He advocates clearer accountability, honest risk assessment, and building robust systems rather than chasing sensational predictions.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为关于‘一切的未来’的宏大叙事往往误导，需要以可验证、务实的技术路径取代。倡导明确的问责、真实的风险评估，以及构建鲁棒系统，而非追捧耸人听闻的预言。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.claudecodecamp.com/p/i-measured-claude-4-7-s-new-tokenizer-here-s-what-it-costs-you" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Measuring Claude 4.7&#x27;s tokenizer costs</span><span class='zh'>测量 Claude 4.7 的分词成本</span></a><span class="dom">claudecodecamp.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A researcher quantifies Claude 4.7&#x27;s tokenizer costs, breaking down per-token economics and per-request implications.</span><span class='zh'>对 Claude 4.7 的分词成本进行量化分析，分解每个 token 的成本与每次请求的经济影响，揭示分词对定价、吞吐量和实际部署效率的作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A researcher quantifies Claude 4.7&#x27;s tokenizer costs, breaking down per-token economics and per-request implications. The analysis shows how tokenization shapes pricing, throughput, and model efficiency in real deployments. Takeaway: tokenization matters as much as model performance when budgeting AI workloads.</span><span class='zh'>对 Claude 4.7 的分词成本进行量化分析，分解每个 token 的成本与每次请求的经济影响，揭示分词对定价、吞吐量和实际部署效率的作用。结论显示，在 AI 工作负载预算中，分词成本与模型性能同等重要。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://ismy.blue/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://ismy.blue/card.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='ismy.blue' data-c='#f472b6' data-tag='Design'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">114</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ismy.blue/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is my blue your blue? (2024)</span><span class='zh'>我的蓝色是你的蓝色吗？</span></a><span class="dom">ismy.blue</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Color perception can vary across displays and lighting; this project experiments with blue to reveal subjective differences in hue naming and perception.</span><span class='zh'>色彩感知在不同显示器和光线下存在差异；该项目用蓝色进行实验，揭示了主观色调命名与感知的差异。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Color perception can vary across displays and lighting; this project experiments with blue to reveal subjective differences in hue naming and perception. The result has implications for UI color choices, accessibility, and cross-device consistency in design.</span><span class='zh'>色彩感知在不同显示器和光线下存在差异；该项目用蓝色进行实验，揭示了主观色调命名与感知的差异。对界面颜色选择、无障碍设计和跨设备的一致性具有启示意义。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://anchor.host/godaddy-gave-a-domain-to-a-stranger-without-any-documentation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://anchor.host/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/godaddy-domain-handover.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anchor.host' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">115</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://anchor.host/godaddy-gave-a-domain-to-a-stranger-without-any-documentation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GoDaddy gave a domain to a stranger without any documentation</span><span class='zh'>GoDaddy 未经文档将域名转让给陌生人</span></a><span class="dom">anchor.host</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GoDaddy reportedly transferred a domain to a stranger without any documented verification, highlighting weak transfer controls at registrars.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，GoDaddy 在未经文档核验的情况下将域名转让给陌生人，暴露出域名转让流程的薄弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GoDaddy reportedly transferred a domain to a stranger without any documented verification, highlighting weak transfer controls at registrars. The incident underscores the risk of ownership confusion and the need for stronger authentication, audit trails, and post-transfer safeguards.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，GoDaddy 在未经文档核验的情况下将域名转让给陌生人，暴露出域名转让流程的薄弱。此事凸显注册商在身份核验与转让审计方面的风险点，并强调需要更强的认证和纠纷处理机制。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">116</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mcdonalds.co.jp/en/menu/burger/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The buns in McDonald&#x27;s Japan&#x27;s burger photos are all slightly askew</span><span class='zh'>日本麥當勞漢堡照片中的麵包略微歪斜</span></a><span class="dom">mcdonalds.co.jp</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A close look at McDonald&#x27;s Japan burger photos reveals buns that are consistently slightly askew.</span><span class='zh'>日本麥當勞的漢堡照片顯示麵包頂部與餡料間常出現輕微不對稱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A close look at McDonald&#x27;s Japan burger photos reveals buns that are consistently slightly askew. The misalignment may be a photography quirk or a production reality, but it sparks discussion about brand presentation versus the actual product. In an era of highly scrutinized visuals, tiny imperfections can become talking points about authenticity in marketing.</span><span class='zh'>日本麥當勞的漢堡照片顯示麵包頂部與餡料間常出現輕微不對稱。這種偏差可能是拍攝風格或製作過程造成，但卻引發對品牌呈現與實物之間差異的討論。在社群媒體高度放大的情境中，這種細微瑕疵也會成為關於真實感的話題。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/emdash-wordpress/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EmDash – A spiritual successor to WordPress that solves plugin security</span><span class='zh'>EmDash：解决插件安全问题的 WordPress 精神续作</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EmDash positions itself as a security-focused successor to WordPress, redesigning plugin architecture to reduce attack surface and improve isolation.</span><span class='zh'>EmDash 将自己定位为以安全为导向的 WordPress 继任者，通过重新设计插件架构与沙箱机制来降低攻击面并提升隔离性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EmDash positions itself as a security-focused successor to WordPress, redesigning plugin architecture to reduce attack surface and improve isolation. By reimagining plugin boundaries, it aims to deliver safer extensibility and better maintainability for developers and site owners.</span><span class='zh'>EmDash 将自己定位为以安全为导向的 WordPress 继任者，通过重新设计插件架构与沙箱机制来降低攻击面并提升隔离性。其目标是在保留插件扩展能力的同时，提高站点的安全性与可维护性，适合注重安全的开发者与站点管理员。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A US special forces soldier was arrested amid allegations of illicit gains linked to a Maduro raid, highlighting potential security and ethics concerns within covert operations.</span><span class='zh'>一名美军特种部队士兵因涉嫌在马杜罗突袭案中获得40万美元收益而被逮捕，暴露了隐秘行动中的治理与伦理问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A US special forces soldier was arrested amid allegations of illicit gains linked to a Maduro raid, highlighting potential security and ethics concerns within covert operations. The case raises questions about governance, accountability, and the handling of sensitive operation proceeds.</span><span class='zh'>一名美军特种部队士兵因涉嫌在马杜罗突袭案中获得40万美元收益而被逮捕，暴露了隐秘行动中的治理与伦理问题。这起案件引发对透明度、问责制以及敏感行动收益处理的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://honeypot.net/2026/04/14/i-wrote-to-flocks-privacy.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I wrote to Flock&#x27;s privacy contact to opt out of their domestic spying program</span><span class='zh'>我给 Flock 的隐私联系邮箱写信，请求退出他们的国内监控计划</span></a><span class="dom">honeypot.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An opt-out request to Flock&#x27;s privacy contact highlights ongoing concerns about how browsers collect data and potential domestic data-sharing arrangements.</span><span class='zh'>向 Flock 的隐私联系渠道提出退出请求，凸显关于浏览器数据收集与潜在国内数据共享的持续担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An opt-out request to Flock&#x27;s privacy contact highlights ongoing concerns about how browsers collect data and potential domestic data-sharing arrangements. The issue underscores the need for transparent privacy controls and auditable telemetry for users who want to protect their data.</span><span class='zh'>向 Flock 的隐私联系渠道提出退出请求，凸显关于浏览器数据收集与潜在国内数据共享的持续担忧。此事强调需要透明的隐私控制与可审计的遥测数据，帮助用户保护自己的信息。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/4/28/before-github/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Before GitHub</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 之前</span></a><span class="dom">lucumr.pocoo.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A retrospective look at collaboration before GitHub, highlighting how developers relied on mailing lists, patches, and other ad-hoc tools.</span><span class='zh'>回顾 GitHub 出现之前的协作方式，揭示开发者如何通过邮件列表、补丁等工具协作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A retrospective look at collaboration before GitHub, highlighting how developers relied on mailing lists, patches, and other ad-hoc tools. The piece contrasts these workflows with modern platforms, emphasizing core needs like code review, issue tracking, and community governance.</span><span class='zh'>回顾 GitHub 出现之前的协作方式，揭示开发者如何通过邮件列表、补丁等工具协作。与现代平台相比，文章强调代码审查、问题跟踪和社区治理等核心需求。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>660</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940921' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>223</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47943499" data-id="47943499" data-tags="Programming" data-score="658" data-comments="359" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47943499" data-time="1777429151">
<a class="thumb" href="https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/social.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='corrode.dev' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">122</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bugs Rust won&#x27;t catch</span><span class='zh'>Rust 捕捉不到的漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">corrode.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rust&#x27;s safety guarantees are strong, but some bugs slip through—particularly around unsafe blocks, FFI, and complex logic that escapes static checks.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 的安全性固然强，但仍有漏洞逃不过静态检查，尤其是涉及 unsafe 代码、外部接口和复杂逻辑的缺陷。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rust&#x27;s safety guarantees are strong, but some bugs slip through—particularly around unsafe blocks, FFI, and complex logic that escapes static checks. The post catalogs typical bugs Rust won&#x27;t catch and explains how to catch them with dynamic testing, fuzzing, and property-based checks. The author emphasizes that safety requires multi-layer defenses, not reliance on the compiler alone.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 的安全性固然强，但仍有漏洞逃不过静态检查，尤其是涉及 unsafe 代码、外部接口和复杂逻辑的缺陷。文章列出 Rust 捕捉不到的常见错误类型，并给出通过动态测试、模糊测试和属性检验等方法来弥补的做法。强调安全需要多层防护，而不仅依赖编译器。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>658</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47943499' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>359</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47739313" data-id="47739313" data-tags="Energy" data-score="657" data-comments="473" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739313" data-time="1776000099">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.the-independent.com/tech/renewable-energy-solar-nepal-bhutan-iceland-b2533699.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://static.the-independent.com/2024/04/16/11/renewable%20energy%20100%20map%20world.png?width=1200&amp;height=800&amp;crop=1200:800' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='the-independent.com' data-c='#eab308' data-tag='Energy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">123</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.the-independent.com/tech/renewable-energy-solar-nepal-bhutan-iceland-b2533699.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Seven countries now generate nearly all their electricity from renewables (2024)</span><span class='zh'>七个国家2024年几乎用可再生能源发电全部电力</span></a><span class="dom">the-independent.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Across several nations, electricity generation is approaching 100% renewables, driven primarily by hydro, wind, and solar plus supportive policy frameworks.</span><span class='zh'>多国的电力生产正在接近100%来自可再生能源，主要靠水电、风电和太阳能及配套政策。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Across several nations, electricity generation is approaching 100% renewables, driven primarily by hydro, wind, and solar plus supportive policy frameworks. While intermittency remains a challenge, these cases show that with grid investments and capacity planning, a transition to low-carbon electricity is increasingly feasible at national scales.</span><span class='zh'>多国的电力生产正在接近100%来自可再生能源，主要靠水电、风电和太阳能及配套政策。尽管间歇性仍是挑战，但这些案例表明通过电网投资和容量规划，国家层面的低碳发电转型正变得更加可行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jack.cab/blog/every-firefox-extension" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Installing every* Firefox extension</span><span class='zh'>安装全部 Firefox 扩展</span></a><span class="dom">jack.cab</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author tests the Firefox extension ecosystem by installing every extension, documenting conflicts, performance, and sanity checks.</span><span class='zh'>作者尝试安装所有 Firefox 扩展，记录冲突、性能与可用性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author tests the Firefox extension ecosystem by installing every extension, documenting conflicts, performance, and sanity checks. The result reveals that the browser&#x27;s extension ecosystem is powerful but can become unwieldy, underscoring the need for better extension vetting and performance budgeting.</span><span class='zh'>作者尝试安装所有 Firefox 扩展，记录冲突、性能与可用性。结果显示扩展生态强大却易变得难以维护，强调需要更严格的扩展筛选与性能预算。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Apr/30/zig-anti-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=simonwillison.net&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>simonwillison.net</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">126</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Apr/30/zig-anti-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Zig project&#x27;s rationale for their anti-AI contribution policy</span><span class='zh'>Zig 对 AI 贡献的明确反对立场及理由</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zig&#x27;s contributor policy explicitly rejects AI-generated code, citing quality, licensing, and accountability concerns.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 的反对 AI 贡献政策明确拒绝 AI 生成的代码，理由包括质量、许可和问责等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zig&#x27;s contributor policy explicitly rejects AI-generated code, citing quality, licensing, and accountability concerns. The policy emphasizes human-authored provenance and review as essential to maintaining code integrity.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 的反对 AI 贡献政策明确拒绝 AI 生成的代码，理由包括质量、许可和问责等问题。该政策强调保持代码的人工源头和人工审查，以确保代码的完整性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://rivian.com/support/article/can-i-disable-all-data-collection-from-my-vehicle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://media.rivian.com/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1695930282/rivian-com/default-riv-com-sharing-image_gjg8mw.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='rivian.com' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rivian.com/support/article/can-i-disable-all-data-collection-from-my-vehicle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Can I disable all data collection from my vehicle?</span><span class='zh'>我能否完全禁用车辆的数据收集？</span></a><span class="dom">rivian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Car makers generally allow opting out of some data collection, but completely disabling telemetry is often not possible due to safety, features, and updates.</span><span class='zh'>多数车企允许关闭某些数据收集，但完全禁用遥测往往因安全性、功能性和更新需求而受限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Car makers generally allow opting out of some data collection, but completely disabling telemetry is often not possible due to safety, features, and updates. The Rivian guidance illustrates what can be turned off and what remains required for core functions like safety and navigation.</span><span class='zh'>多数车企允许关闭某些数据收集，但完全禁用遥测往往因安全性、功能性和更新需求而受限。Rivian 的指南显示哪些数据可以关闭、哪些仍然被收集用于安全与导航等核心功能。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://essays.johnloeber.com/p/4-bring-back-idiomatic-design" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!o7Gx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1d9c4a86-cb01-4f2c-ab0e-1cf2f0d0a2c8_419x304.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='essays.johnloeber.com' data-c='#f472b6' data-tag='Design'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://essays.johnloeber.com/p/4-bring-back-idiomatic-design" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bring Back Idiomatic Design (2023)</span><span class='zh'>回归地道设计</span></a><span class="dom">essays.johnloeber.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues for designing APIs and UIs that follow the host language&#x27;s idioms rather than importing external design paradigms.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在软件设计中遵循目标语言的习惯用法，而非照搬外部风格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues for designing APIs and UIs that follow the host language&#x27;s idioms rather than importing external design paradigms. It reinforces how idiomatic design improves readability and safety.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在软件设计中遵循目标语言的习惯用法，而非照搬外部风格。强调地道设计提升可读性、安全性和开发者体验。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://asteriskmag.com/issues/14/the-mystery-in-the-medicine-cabinet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/14/the-mystery-in-the-medicine-cabinet/e71f2c3954-1776281832/dynomight-social-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='asteriskmag.com' data-c='#4ade80' data-tag='Health'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">129</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://asteriskmag.com/issues/14/the-mystery-in-the-medicine-cabinet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Acetaminophen vs. ibuprofen</span><span class='zh'>对乙酰氨基酚 vs. 布洛芬</span></a><span class="dom">asteriskmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Acetaminophen and ibuprofen treat different types of pain and have distinct risk profiles.</span><span class='zh'>对乙酰氨基酚和布洛芬在机制与风险上各有不同。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Acetaminophen and ibuprofen treat different types of pain and have distinct risk profiles. Acetaminophen is gentler on the stomach but can damage the liver at high doses, while ibuprofen reduces inflammation but may upset the stomach and affect kidney function; dosing and medical context matter for safe use.</span><span class='zh'>对乙酰氨基酚和布洛芬在机制与风险上各有不同。乙酰氨基酚对胃部影响较小，但高剂量可能损伤肝脏；布洛芬具有抗炎作用，但可能引起胃部不适并影响肾功能，使用时需考虑炎症程度、年龄和肝肾健康。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Health' style='--c:#4ade80'>Health</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>646</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835635' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>427</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47965060" data-id="47965060" data-tags="Privacy|Politics" data-score="639" data-comments="215" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965060" data-time="1777567260">
<a class="thumb" href="https://thereader.mitpress.mit.edu/the-whistleblower-who-uncovered-the-nsas-big-brother-machine/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=thereader.mitpress.mit.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>thereader.mitpress.mit.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">130</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thereader.mitpress.mit.edu/the-whistleblower-who-uncovered-the-nsas-big-brother-machine/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How Mark Klein told the EFF about Room 641A [book excerpt]</span><span class='zh'>马克·克莱因向 EFF 披露641A室内幕的书摘</span></a><span class="dom">thereader.mitpress.mit.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mark Klein, a former AT&amp;T technician, disclosed to the EFF the existence of Room 641A, a secret facility where fiber-optic lines were allegedly tapped for mass surveillance.</span><span class='zh'>前 AT&amp;T 技术员 Mark Klein 向电子前哨基金会披露了“641A 室”的存在，据称在那里对光纤线路进行了大规模监控拦截。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mark Klein, a former AT&amp;T technician, disclosed to the EFF the existence of Room 641A, a secret facility where fiber-optic lines were allegedly tapped for mass surveillance. The excerpt presents his firsthand account and the ensuing controversy, showing how insider disclosures can catalyze public scrutiny and policy debate around surveillance. It underscores the lasting impact whistleblowers have on privacy advocacy and reform efforts.</span><span class='zh'>前 AT&amp;T 技术员 Mark Klein 向电子前哨基金会披露了“641A 室”的存在，据称在那里对光纤线路进行了大规模监控拦截。书摘给出他的第一手叙述及随后的争议，展示了内部披露如何点燃公众关注并推动 surveillance 相关的政策辩论。此事凸显举报人在隐私倡议与改革中的长期影响。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>639</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965060' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>215</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47909226" data-id="47909226" data-tags="Open Source|Hardware" data-score="630" data-comments="323" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47909226" data-time="1777200632">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 pushes forward with improvements to ARM Mac compatibility, earlier boot issues, and new developer tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 在 ARM Mac 兼容性、早期启动问题和开发工具方面取得改进，持续缩小 Apple Silicon 与开源 Linux 之间的差距。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 pushes forward with improvements to ARM Mac compatibility, earlier boot issues, and new developer tooling. The project continues narrowing the gap between Apple Silicon hardware and open-source Linux.</span><span class='zh'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 在 ARM Mac 兼容性、早期启动问题和开发工具方面取得改进，持续缩小 Apple Silicon 与开源 Linux 之间的差距。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213#issuecomment-4347988313" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">132</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213#issuecomment-4347988313" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mozilla&#x27;s opposition to Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 反对 Chrome 的 Prompt API</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mozilla has voiced concerns about Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API, arguing it could complicate user consent and privacy controls and fragment browser standards.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 对 Chrome 的 Prompt API 表态反对，担心会削弱用户同意与隐私控制，并破坏跨浏览器标准的一致性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mozilla has voiced concerns about Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API, arguing it could complicate user consent and privacy controls and fragment browser standards. The stance highlights ongoing tensions between platform features, privacy safeguards, and interoperability in browser ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 对 Chrome 的 Prompt API 表态反对，担心会削弱用户同意与隐私控制，并破坏跨浏览器标准的一致性。这一立场凸显了浏览器生态中对隐私保护、功能设计与互操作性的持续博弈。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>626</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47959463' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>224</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47788385" data-id="47788385" data-tags="Programming|AI" data-score="626" data-comments="207" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788385" data-time="1776310511">
<a class="thumb" href="https://sleepingrobots.com/dreams/stop-using-ollama/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://sleepingrobots.com/og.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sleepingrobots.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">133</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sleepingrobots.com/dreams/stop-using-ollama/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The local LLM ecosystem doesn’t need Ollama</span><span class='zh'>本地大模型生态不需要 Ollama</span></a><span class="dom">sleepingrobots.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ollama is not essential for local LLM workflows; the piece argues that the local ecosystem can be served by simpler tooling or native inference stacks, reducing friction and maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>本文认为本地大语言模型生态并不需要 Ollama；可以通过更简单的工具或原生推理栈完成同样任务，降低运维成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ollama is not essential for local LLM workflows; the piece argues that the local ecosystem can be served by simpler tooling or native inference stacks, reducing friction and maintenance. It weighs trade-offs in updates, platform compatibility, and ecosystem maturity.</span><span class='zh'>本文认为本地大语言模型生态并不需要 Ollama；可以通过更简单的工具或原生推理栈完成同样任务，降低运维成本。文章比较了更新、平台兼容性和生态成熟度等权衡，指出 Ollama 可能并非必需。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://arhan.sh/blog/native-instant-space-switching-on-macos/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f472b6' data-tag='Design'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=arhan.sh&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>arhan.sh</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arhan.sh/blog/native-instant-space-switching-on-macos/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Native Instant Space Switching on macOS</span><span class='zh'>macOS 原生即时空间切换</span></a><span class="dom">arhan.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>macOS now supports native instant switching between virtual desktops (Spaces), drastically reducing the perceived latency when moving between spaces.</span><span class='zh'>macOS 现在原生支持在虚拟桌面（Spaces）之间即时切换，显著降低切换时的延迟，提升多任务处理体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>macOS now supports native instant switching between virtual desktops (Spaces), drastically reducing the perceived latency when moving between spaces. It highlights implications for workflow efficiency, window management, and potential limitations compared with third-party tools.</span><span class='zh'>macOS 现在原生支持在虚拟桌面（Spaces）之间即时切换，显著降低切换时的延迟，提升多任务处理体验。文中讨论了这一特性对窗口管理和工作流程的影响，以及与第三方工具相比的潜在局限。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">135</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.axios.com/2026/04/09/meta-social-media-addiction-ads" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta removes ads for social media addiction litigation</span><span class='zh'>Meta因社媒成瘾诉讼而移除广告</span></a><span class="dom">axios.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta&#x27;s move to remove ads linked to a social-media addiction lawsuit underscores how litigation is nudging platforms to rethink ad-supported models and user-well-being commitments.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 因社媒成瘾诉讼而移除相关广告，凸显法律诉讼正在推动平台重新审视广告支持模式与对用户健康的承诺。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta&#x27;s move to remove ads linked to a social-media addiction lawsuit underscores how litigation is nudging platforms to rethink ad-supported models and user-well-being commitments. The case could ripple through advertising strategies and policy discussions around platform responsibility.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 因社媒成瘾诉讼而移除相关广告，凸显法律诉讼正在推动平台重新审视广告支持模式与对用户健康的承诺。此举可能影响广告策略与对平台责任的政策讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://theonion.com/at-long-last-infowars-is-ours/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>At long last, InfoWars is ours</span><span class='zh'>InfoWars 终于落入我们手中</span></a><span class="dom">theonion.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A satirical piece imagines someone finally taking control of InfoWars, lampooning the power dynamics of fringe media and online info ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>这篇讽刺文设想有人终于掌控 InfoWars，调侃边缘媒体的权力关系与信息生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A satirical piece imagines someone finally taking control of InfoWars, lampooning the power dynamics of fringe media and online info ecosystems. It skewers how ownership can shape narratives and the culture around conspiracy sites, offering a meta take on media influence.</span><span class='zh'>这篇讽刺文设想有人终于掌控 InfoWars，调侃边缘媒体的权力关系与信息生态。通过字句讽刺所有权如何塑造叙事，以及公众对阴谋论媒体的看法，折射出信息环境中的权力与影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ollama.com/blog/mlx" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ollama is now powered by MLX on Apple Silicon in preview</span><span class='zh'>Ollama 在 Apple Silicon 上开启 MLX 预览加速</span></a><span class="dom">ollama.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ollama now supports MLX on Apple Silicon in preview, enabling accelerated on-device LLMs on M-series Macs.</span><span class='zh'>Ollama 现已在 Apple Silicon 支持 MLX，提供本地推理的加速效果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ollama now supports MLX on Apple Silicon in preview, enabling accelerated on-device LLMs on M-series Macs. The preview could bring notable speedups and privacy benefits versus cloud inference, but adopters should be aware of early-stage status and hardware compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>Ollama 现已在 Apple Silicon 支持 MLX，提供本地推理的加速效果。预览版可能带来显著的性能提升和隐私优势，但仍处于早期阶段，需关注兼容性与稳定性。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">138</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01193" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Embarrassingly simple self-distillation improves code generation</span><span class='zh'>苹果：极其简单的自蒸馏提升代码生成</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A simple self-distillation technique improves code generation quality in experiments, suggesting a cheap method to boost model performance without large compute overhead.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示一种极其简单的自蒸馏方法就能提升代码生成质量，成本低且易于在现有模型上实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A simple self-distillation technique improves code generation quality in experiments, suggesting a cheap method to boost model performance without large compute overhead. It could be applied to training or inference of code assistants.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示一种极其简单的自蒸馏方法就能提升代码生成质量，成本低且易于在现有模型上实现。该方法对提升代码生成能力有潜在意义，可能为改进代码助手和编程相关任务提供可行路径。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://cacm.acm.org/news/how-nasa-built-artemis-iis-fault-tolerant-computer/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#818cf8' data-tag='Space'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=cacm.acm.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>cacm.acm.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">139</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://cacm.acm.org/news/how-nasa-built-artemis-iis-fault-tolerant-computer/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How NASA built Artemis II’s fault-tolerant computer</span><span class='zh'>NASA 如何打造 Artemis II 的容错计算机</span></a><span class="dom">cacm.acm.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NASA built a fault-tolerant onboard computer for Artemis II, emphasizing resilience in radiation-rich space.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号任务的机载容错计算机采用冗余、错误检测和抗辐射组件等技术，以在关键硬件失效时仍能维持运作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NASA built a fault-tolerant onboard computer for Artemis II, emphasizing resilience in radiation-rich space. The system relies on redundancy, error detection, and hardened components to keep crewed flight safe even if primary hardware fails. The design illustrates how space-grade computing priorities differ from consumer systems.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号任务的机载容错计算机采用冗余、错误检测和抗辐射组件等技术，以在关键硬件失效时仍能维持运作。该系统体现了太空计算对可靠性的高要求，远超商用系统的容错能力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Space' style='--c:#818cf8'>Space</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>616</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47704804' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>222</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47606840" data-id="47606840" data-tags="Hardware" data-score="614" data-comments="529" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47606840" data-time="1775079401">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/dram-pricing-is-killing-the-hobbyist-sbc-market/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DRAM pricing is killing the hobbyist SBC market</span><span class='zh'>DRAM定价正在挤压爱好者级SBC市场</span></a><span class="dom">jeffgeerling.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rising DRAM prices are squeezing the hobbyist SBC market, where memory is a core cost.</span><span class='zh'>DRAM价格上涨挤压了爱好者级SBC市场，内存成本成为关键负担。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rising DRAM prices are squeezing the hobbyist SBC market, where memory is a core cost. As memory becomes a larger share of BOM, many affordable boards struggle on margins and demand. The shift could push makers toward leaner designs or pre-bundled solutions.</span><span class='zh'>DRAM价格上涨挤压了爱好者级SBC市场，内存成本成为关键负担。随着内存占比上升，许多平价开发板的利润率和需求都被压缩。市场动荡可能促使厂商推出更节省内存的设计或打包解决方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/10/france-to-ditch-windows-for-linux-to-reduce-reliance-on-us-tech/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>France to ditch Windows for Linux to reduce reliance on US tech</span><span class='zh'>法国将放弃 Windows 改用 Linux，降低对美国科技的依赖</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>France plans to replace Windows with Linux on government devices to reduce reliance on US tech and tighten sovereignty.</span><span class='zh'>法国计划把政府设备从 Windows 切换到 Linux，以减少对美国科技的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>France plans to replace Windows with Linux on government devices to reduce reliance on US tech and tighten sovereignty. The move signals growing appetite for open-source in public sector procurement and raises questions about migration complexity and vendor ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>法国计划把政府设备从 Windows 切换到 Linux，以减少对美国科技的依赖。这一举措凸显公共部门对开源软件的越来越大兴趣，同时也带来迁移的挑战与生态影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GLM-5.1 advances long-horizon reasoning, aiming to improve task planning and sustained context handling in large language models.</span><span class='zh'>GLM-5.1 在长期推理能力上取得进展，提升任务规划与长上下文处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GLM-5.1 advances long-horizon reasoning, aiming to improve task planning and sustained context handling in large language models. The update signals continued the trend toward more capable multi-step problem solving, useful for complex workflows.</span><span class='zh'>GLM-5.1 在长期推理能力上取得进展，提升任务规划与长上下文处理。该更新延续了让大型语言模型进行多步推理、处理复杂任务的趋势，对需要跨长文档的应用尤为有用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tokens.billchambers.me/leaderboard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anonymous request-token comparisons from Opus 4.6 and Opus 4.7</span><span class='zh'>Opus 4.6 与 Opus 4.7 的匿名请求令牌比较</span></a><span class="dom">tokens.billchambers.me</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A public leaderboard contrasts how many tokens are consumed by anonymous requests across Opus 4.6 and Opus 4.7. The results illustrate how a version bump can change token efficiency, with practical implications for cost-conscious API users and performance tuning.</span><span class='zh'>公开的排行榜对比 Opus 4.6 与 4.7 在匿名请求中消耗的令牌数量，揭示版本迭代对 token 效率的影响。对希望控制成本和优化性能的开发者来说，这类比较提供了有价值的基准。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://bramcohen.com/p/the-cult-of-vibe-coding-is-insane" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#d946ef' data-tag='Culture'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=bramcohen.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>bramcohen.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">146</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bramcohen.com/p/the-cult-of-vibe-coding-is-insane" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The cult of vibe coding is dogfooding run amok</span><span class='zh'>氛围编码的崇拜：自我喂养文化失控</span></a><span class="dom">bramcohen.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that &#x27;vibe coding&#x27;—prioritizing aesthetics, culture, and vibes over engineering rigor—has become a dogfooding trap, where teams use internal satisfaction as a proxy for quality.</span><span class='zh'>本文批评了所谓的“氛围编码”现象，强调注重美学与氛围而忽视工程严谨的趋势成为自我喂养的陷阱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that &#x27;vibe coding&#x27;—prioritizing aesthetics, culture, and vibes over engineering rigor—has become a dogfooding trap, where teams use internal satisfaction as a proxy for quality. It warns such culture can erode product reliability, real user outcomes, and long-run sustainability. The takeaway is to balance vibe with substance and user needs.</span><span class='zh'>本文批评了所谓的“氛围编码”现象，强调注重美学与氛围而忽视工程严谨的趋势成为自我喂养的陷阱。内部测试和满足感被当成质量指标，可能侵蚀产品可靠性、真实用户价值与长期可持续性。核心是要在氛围和实质、用户需求之间取得平衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Aliens discover humans are meat and struggle to understand Earth&#x27;s beings; the story uses a stark premise to question assumptions about life, intelligence, and cross-cultural communication.</span><span class='zh'>外星人发现人类其实是肉体所构成，他们努力理解地球生命与意识的边界。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Aliens discover humans are meat and struggle to understand Earth&#x27;s beings; the story uses a stark premise to question assumptions about life, intelligence, and cross-cultural communication. It remains a sharp, classic piece on perspective.</span><span class='zh'>外星人发现人类其实是肉体所构成，他们努力理解地球生命与意识的边界。通过简练又讽刺的情节，质疑关于生命、智能与跨文化沟通的假设。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An F-15E was shot down over Iran, marking a significant escalation in regional tensions.</span><span class='zh'>一架 F-15E 战斗机在伊朗上空被击落，标志着区域紧张局势的升级。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An F-15E was shot down over Iran, marking a significant escalation in regional tensions. The incident underscores the fragility of aviation assets in volatile zones and the risk of broader security spillovers. It will likely influence defense Posture and policy considerations.</span><span class='zh'>一架 F-15E 战斗机在伊朗上空被击落，标志着区域紧张局势的升级。此事件凸显在高风险地区维护航空资产的脆弱性与潜在的安全扩散风险，可能影响防务态势与政策取向。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mining.com/france-pulls-last-gold-held-in-us-for-15b-gain/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>France pulls last gold held in US</span><span class='zh'>法国从美国取回最后一批黄金，获利约150亿美元</span></a><span class="dom">mining.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>France moved its remaining gold reserves from the United States, realizing about $15 billion in gains.</span><span class='zh'>法国将其在美黄金储备中的最后一部分取回，实现约150亿美元的收益。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>France moved its remaining gold reserves from the United States, realizing about $15 billion in gains. The move underscores ongoing central-bank diversification away from custody risk and signals geopolitical considerations in asset custody. It also highlights how precious metals markets react to sovereign reserve shifts.</span><span class='zh'>法国将其在美黄金储备中的最后一部分取回，实现约150亿美元的收益。这显示央行在分散储备时继续降低对单一托管方的依赖，并反映了资产保管的地缘政治考量。除此之外，此类举动往往会影响贵金属市场的供需和价格走向。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://9to5mac.com/2026/04/09/fbi-used-iphone-notification-data-to-retrieve-deleted-signal-messages/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FBI used iPhone notification data to retrieve deleted Signal messages</span><span class='zh'>FBI 使用 iPhone 通知数据检索已删除的 Signal 消息</span></a><span class="dom">9to5mac.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Forensic use of iPhone notification data enabled recovery of deleted Signal messages, highlighting how notification metadata can bypass user deletions.</span><span class='zh'>通过分析 iPhone 通知数据，联邦调查局帮助找回了已删除的 Signal 消息，暴露了通知元数据可能绕过删除保护的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Forensic use of iPhone notification data enabled recovery of deleted Signal messages, highlighting how notification metadata can bypass user deletions. This underscores a growing privacy and security risk for messaging apps, and could intensify debates over data minimization and platform protections.</span><span class='zh'>通过分析 iPhone 通知数据，联邦调查局帮助找回了已删除的 Signal 消息，暴露了通知元数据可能绕过删除保护的风险。此事凸显隐私与安全之间的博弈，或将推动关于数据最小化与平台保护的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.tangled.org/federation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We need a federation of forges</span><span class='zh'>Tangled：我们需要一个联邦制的代码托管平台网络</span></a><span class="dom">blog.tangled.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tangled argues for a federation of code forges to avoid vendor lock-in and improve collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>Tangled 主张建立一个联邦制的代码托管平台网络，以避免厂商锁定并提升协作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tangled argues for a federation of code forges to avoid vendor lock-in and improve collaboration. It sketches a model for interoperable identities, permissions, and data exchange across platforms, with governance considerations for a healthy ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>Tangled 主张建立一个联邦制的代码托管平台网络，以避免厂商锁定并提升协作。文中勾画了在各个平台间实现互操作身份、权限与数据交换的模型，并就健康生态的治理提出思路。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://simonomi.dev/blog/color-code-your-bytes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://simonomi.dev/images/color-code-your-bytes/preview.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='simonomi.dev' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonomi.dev/blog/color-code-your-bytes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Your hex editor should color-code bytes</span><span class='zh'>你的十六进制编辑器应对字节进行颜色编码</span></a><span class="dom">simonomi.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A post argues color-coding bytes in hex editors improves readability and reduces mistakes when inspecting binary data.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在十六进制编辑器中对字节进行颜色编码，以提升可读性并降低读错的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A post argues color-coding bytes in hex editors improves readability and reduces mistakes when inspecting binary data. It discusses schemes for distinguishing text vs binary regions, endianness, and pattern detection.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在十六进制编辑器中对字节进行颜色编码，以提升可读性并降低读错的风险。它提出将 ASCII 区域、二进制数据、常见魔数等用不同颜色标示，以及如何辅助识别模式和字节序等细节。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/new-10-gbe-usb-adapters-cooler-smaller-cheaper/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/new-10-gbe-usb-adapters-cooler-smaller-cheaper/thunderbolt-and-usb-c-10g-ethernet-adapters.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='jeffgeerling.com' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/new-10-gbe-usb-adapters-cooler-smaller-cheaper/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New 10 GbE USB adapters are cooler, smaller, cheaper</span><span class='zh'>新款 10 GbE USB 适配器更酷更小更便宜</span></a><span class="dom">jeffgeerling.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New 10GbE USB adapters bring more affordable, compact, and practical options for high-speed network expansion.</span><span class='zh'>新款 10GbE USB 适配器体积更小、价格更低，降低了在家用实验室、媒体服务器等场景扩展高端网络的门槛。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New 10GbE USB adapters bring more affordable, compact, and practical options for high-speed network expansion. They lower the barrier for homelabs, media servers, and USB-C powered devices to add fast Ethernet without PCIe NICs. Consider driver support and power draw when choosing a model.</span><span class='zh'>新款 10GbE USB 适配器体积更小、价格更低，降低了在家用实验室、媒体服务器等场景扩展高端网络的门槛。无需 PCIe NIC 就能实现千兆级别的以太网速率，适合搭配 USB-C 设备使用。选购时留意驱动支持和供电需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.hypertalking.com/2023/05/08/1-bit-pixel-art-of-hokusais-the-great-wave-off-kanagawa/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>1-Bit Hokusai&#x27;s &quot;The Great Wave&quot; (2023)</span><span class='zh'>1 位元像素風格的《神奈川沖浪裏》（2023）</span></a><span class="dom">hypertalking.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 1-bit pixel-art reinterpretation of Hokusai&#x27;s The Great Wave Off Kanagawa, created in 2023.</span><span class='zh'>用1位元像素風格重新詮釋葛飾北斎的《神奈川沖浪裏》，作品於2023年亮相。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 1-bit pixel-art reinterpretation of Hokusai&#x27;s The Great Wave Off Kanagawa, created in 2023. The piece demonstrates how ultra-minimal digital art can echo a masterwork&#x27;s motion and drama, pushing the boundaries of what &#x27;low fidelity&#x27; can convey. It underscores the enduring appeal of classic images in the era of retro aesthetics and generative art.</span><span class='zh'>用1位元像素風格重新詮釋葛飾北斎的《神奈川沖浪裏》，作品於2023年亮相。極簡像素的限制放大波濤的動勢與張力，顯示經典名作在現代數位美學中的再創造力與吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://playstarfling.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Starfling: A one-tap endless orbital slingshot game in a single HTML file</span><span class='zh'>Starfling：一个 HTML 单文件的无尽轨道弹射游戏</span></a><span class="dom">playstarfling.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Starfling is a minimalist browser game playable in a single HTML file, using a tap to control orbital slingshots.</span><span class='zh'>Starfling 是一个极简的浏览器游戏，直接在单个 HTML 文件中就能运行，点击一次即可进行轨道弹射。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Starfling is a minimalist browser game playable in a single HTML file, using a tap to control orbital slingshots. It showcases how far a tiny, self-contained web app can push interactivity and visuals with pure HTML/JS, without a build or asset pipeline. A fun example of ultra-lightweight game development and portability.</span><span class='zh'>Starfling 是一个极简的浏览器游戏，直接在单个 HTML 文件中就能运行，点击一次即可进行轨道弹射。它演示了使用纯 HTML/JS 就能实现丰富交互与流畅画面的极致轻量化游戏，并且无需构建工具即可带来完整体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://letsdatascience.com/news/atlassian-enables-default-data-collection-to-train-ai-f71343d8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Atlassian enables default data collection to train AI</span><span class='zh'>Atlassian 启用默认数据收集以训练 AI</span></a><span class="dom">letsdatascience.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Atlassian plans to enable default data collection to train AI across its product suite, accelerating model improvements through user interactions.</span><span class='zh'>Atlassian 计划在其产品套件中默认收集数据用于训练 AI，从而通过用户交互来加速模型改进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Atlassian plans to enable default data collection to train AI across its product suite, accelerating model improvements through user interactions. The move boosts AI-powered features but raises privacy, data governance, and policy questions for enterprises.</span><span class='zh'>Atlassian 计划在其产品套件中默认收集数据用于训练 AI，从而通过用户交互来加速模型改进。这提升了 AI 驱动的功能，但也引发隐私、数据治理与企业政策方面的担忧。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reports indicate Microsoft terminated a VeraCrypt account, halting Windows updates.</span><span class='zh'>报道称微软突然终止了VeraCrypt账户，导致Windows更新中断。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reports indicate Microsoft terminated a VeraCrypt account, halting Windows updates. The incident underscores how dependence on external accounts can disrupt critical software maintenance and raises questions about vendor policies.</span><span class='zh'>报道称微软突然终止了VeraCrypt账户，导致Windows更新中断。这一事件凸显对外部账户的依赖如何影响关键软件维护，并引发对厂商政策的关注。</span></p>

The update likely improves grounding, reliability, and multi-step problem solving for practical tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen3.6-Plus 迈向现实世界的智能代理，体现从单纯对话型模型向工具使用和多步推理更稳健的转变。该更新大概率提升模型的落地性、稳定性，以及与外部工具的集成能力。</span></p>

The video traces memory cells and timing while discussing the challenges of building RAM-like circuitry at home, offering a tangible look at memory architecture.</span><span class='zh'>该视频用家用组件演示一个 RAM 原型，帮助理解内存如何工作。它讲解存储单元、时序，以及在家自制 RAM 相关电路所遇到的挑战，直观展现了内存架构的要点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://app.oravys.com/blog/mercor-breach-2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>4TB of voice samples just stolen from 40k AI contractors at Mercor</span><span class='zh'>Mercor 的外包承包商数据泄露：4 TB 语音样本被窃取</span></a><span class="dom">app.oravys.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mercor suffered a breach exposing 4TB of voice samples from about 40,000 AI contractors.</span><span class='zh'>Mercor 的安全事件暴露了来自约4万名 AI 合作者的4 TB 语音样本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mercor suffered a breach exposing 4TB of voice samples from about 40,000 AI contractors. The incident underscores the privacy and security risks in outsourced data-labeling workflows and could fuel debates about consent, governance, and stronger data handling controls.</span><span class='zh'>Mercor 的安全事件暴露了来自约4万名 AI 合作者的4 TB 语音样本。此事凸显外包数据标注流程中的隐私与安全风险，或促使对数据授权、治理与更强数据处理控制的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.skoda-storyboard.com/en/skoda-world/skoda-duobell-a-bicycle-bell-that-outsmarts-even-smart-headphones/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Škoda DuoBell: A bicycle bell that penetrates noise-cancelling headphones</span><span class='zh'>Škoda DuoBell：一枚能穿透降噪耳机的自行车铃</span></a><span class="dom">skoda-storyboard.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Škoda&#x27;s DuoBell uses an audible signal designed to cut through active noise cancellation, ensuring cyclists are noticed.</span><span class='zh'>Škoda 的 DuoBell 设计成在主动降噪耳机下仍能发出清晰声音，确保骑行者被听见。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Škoda&#x27;s DuoBell uses an audible signal designed to cut through active noise cancellation, ensuring cyclists are noticed. It highlights practical hardware design for urban mobility and how audible cues interact with consumer audio tech.</span><span class='zh'>Škoda 的 DuoBell 设计成在主动降噪耳机下仍能发出清晰声音，确保骑行者被听见。该产品凸显城市出行的实用硬件设计，以及可听信号与消费级音频技术的互动。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://rdi.berkeley.edu/blog/trustworthy-benchmarks-cont/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=rdi.berkeley.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>rdi.berkeley.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">164</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rdi.berkeley.edu/blog/trustworthy-benchmarks-cont/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Exploiting the most prominent AI agent benchmarks</span><span class='zh'>利用最显著的 AI 代理基准测试</span></a><span class="dom">rdi.berkeley.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Berkeley piece critiques how prominent AI benchmarks are exploited or gamed, questioning reliability of current benchmarks for real-world agent evaluation.</span><span class='zh'>Berkeley 的文章质疑当前最显著的 AI 代理基准的健壮性，指出评测结果可能被操纵或不充分反映现实场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Berkeley piece critiques how prominent AI benchmarks are exploited or gamed, questioning reliability of current benchmarks for real-world agent evaluation. It argues for more robust, trustworthy benchmarks to guide research and product decisions.</span><span class='zh'>Berkeley 的文章质疑当前最显著的 AI 代理基准的健壮性，指出评测结果可能被操纵或不充分反映现实场景。它呼吁建立更可信的基准，以正确引导研究方向和产品决策。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-copilot/for-individuals/termsofuse" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft: Copilot is for entertainment purposes only</span><span class='zh'>微软：Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途</span></a><span class="dom">microsoft.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft explicitly states in its terms that Copilot is for entertainment purposes only, clarifying its intended use and limitations.</span><span class='zh'>微软在使用条款中明确表示 Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途，界定了其使用范围与局限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft explicitly states in its terms that Copilot is for entertainment purposes only, clarifying its intended use and limitations. This stance raises questions about relying on AI for critical coding or decision-making and highlights the need for clear risk disclosures.</span><span class='zh'>微软在使用条款中明确表示 Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途，界定了其使用范围与局限。此说法引发了人们对将其用于关键编码或决策场景的可依赖性以及需披露的风险信息的关注。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">166</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://archive.org/details/byte-magazine-1975-09" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Archive of BYTE magazine, starting with issue #1 in 1975</span><span class='zh'>Byte 杂志档案，起始于1975年第1期</span></a><span class="dom">archive.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Archive of Byte magazine from 1975 onward provides a window into early microcomputer culture.</span><span class='zh'>Byte 杂志档案为人们打开了1970年代末至1980年代初计算机文化的窗口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Archive of Byte magazine from 1975 onward provides a window into early microcomputer culture. It collects hardware hacks, software reviews, and stories that shaped early personal computing. For historians and tinkerers, it&#x27;s a valuable snapshot of the era&#x27;s ideas and constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Byte 杂志档案为人们打开了1970年代末至1980年代初计算机文化的窗口。它汇集了硬件 hacks、软件评测以及塑造早期个人计算的故事。对于历史研究者和动手爱好者来说，这是了解那个时代思想与局限性的宝贵快照。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scottlawsonbc.com/post/dot-system" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A dot a day keeps the clutter away</span><span class='zh'>每天一个点，清除杂乱</span></a><span class="dom">scottlawsonbc.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47906253" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: An app is silently installing itself on my iPhone every day</span><span class='zh'>Tell HN：每天都有应用悄悄在 iPhone 上安装</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A user reports a mystery app that installs itself daily on iPhone, prompting questions about how apps gain such persistence and what privacy controls failed.</span><span class='zh'>用户报告每天在 iPhone 上悄悄新增安装的应用，引发关于应用如何获得持续权限与隐私控制失效的质疑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A user reports a mystery app that installs itself daily on iPhone, prompting questions about how apps gain such persistence and what privacy controls failed. The thread highlights gaps in iOS security storytelling and urges stronger app review and user protections.</span><span class='zh'>用户报告每天在 iPhone 上悄悄新增安装的应用，引发关于应用如何获得持续权限与隐私控制失效的质疑。讨论聚焦 iOS 安全边界的薄弱点，呼吁加强应用审核和用户保护。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://updates.thunderbird.net/en-US/thunderbird/140.0/apr26-1e/donate/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://updates.thunderbird.net/media/img/thunderbird/thunderbird-256.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='updates.thunderbird.net' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">173</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://updates.thunderbird.net/en-US/thunderbird/140.0/apr26-1e/donate/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Help Keep Thunderbird Alive</span><span class='zh'>帮助 Thunderbird 项目继续存在</span></a><span class="dom">updates.thunderbird.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Thunderbird, the open-source email client, is running a fundraising drive to stay active.</span><span class='zh'>开源邮件客户端 Thunderbird 正发起募捐以维持项目运营。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Thunderbird, the open-source email client, is running a fundraising drive to stay active. The appeal underscores ongoing challenges for volunteer-driven projects in sustaining development and support, even as mainstream clients rise. Donating helps ensure continued updates, privacy features, and localization support for users who value control over their data.</span><span class='zh'>开源邮件客户端 Thunderbird 正发起募捐以维持项目运营。社区呼吁捐助以解决长期资金与维护挑战，确保未来仍具备更新、隐私功能及本地化支持。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.derekthompson.org/p/if-americas-so-rich-howd-it-get-so" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!gAKe!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F16200edd-4e29-4729-8d58-4eefbab120d8_1260x893.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='derekthompson.org' data-c='#f97316' data-tag='Politics'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">174</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.derekthompson.org/p/if-americas-so-rich-howd-it-get-so" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If America&#x27;s so rich, how&#x27;d it get so sad?</span><span class='zh'>美国这么富有，怎会变得如此悲伤？</span></a><span class="dom">derekthompson.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Derek Thompson argues that America&#x27;s wealth hasn&#x27;t translated into happiness due to inequality, social fragmentation, and policy choices; the piece connects economic growth with wellbeing and the impact of technology like AI.</span><span class='zh'>德里克·汤普森认为美国的繁荣并未带来相应的幸福感，原因在于不平等、社会分化和政策选择。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Derek Thompson argues that America&#x27;s wealth hasn&#x27;t translated into happiness due to inequality, social fragmentation, and policy choices; the piece connects economic growth with wellbeing and the impact of technology like AI.</span><span class='zh'>德里克·汤普森认为美国的繁荣并未带来相应的幸福感，原因在于不平等、社会分化和政策选择。文章探讨经济增长、社会福祉之间的错配，以及技术（包括 AI）对这种关系的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nldigitalgovernment.nl/news/soft-launch-for-government-open-source-code-platform/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Soft launch of open-source code platform for government</span><span class='zh'>政府开放源代码平台进行软启动</span></a><span class="dom">nldigitalgovernment.nl</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A government-backed open-source code platform is entering a soft launch, inviting developers to contribute and test components for public-sector software.</span><span class='zh'>由政府牵头的开源代码平台进入软启动阶段，邀请开发者参与测试和贡献。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A government-backed open-source code platform is entering a soft launch, inviting developers to contribute and test components for public-sector software. The initiative aims to accelerate modernization, improve security through collaborative development, and reduce vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>由政府牵头的开源代码平台进入软启动阶段，邀请开发者参与测试和贡献。此举旨在加速公共部门软件现代化、提升安全性并减少厂商锁定。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.openwall.com/lists/oss-security/2026/04/30/10" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>For Linux kernel vulnerabilities, there is no heads-up to distributions</span><span class='zh'>Linux 内核漏洞：对发行版没有事先通知</span></a><span class="dom">openwall.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Linux kernel vulnerability disclosures often fail to provide a formal heads-up to distributions, delaying patch adoption.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 内核漏洞的披露往往没有直接向发行版提供正式的事先通知，导致修补落地延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Linux kernel vulnerability disclosures often fail to provide a formal heads-up to distributions, delaying patch adoption. The situation highlights gaps in how advisories propagate from researchers to downstream users, affecting enterprise risk management.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 内核漏洞的披露往往没有直接向发行版提供正式的事先通知，导致修补落地延迟。这暴露了研究人员、公告渠道与发行版维护之间的信息传递断层，影响企业的风险管理和修补节奏。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WireGuard released a Windows build aligned with a Microsoft signing resolution, addressing long-standing driver-signing hurdles.</span><span class='zh'>在微软签名决议推动下，WireGuard 推出新的 Windows 版本，解决长期的驱动签名问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WireGuard released a Windows build aligned with a Microsoft signing resolution, addressing long-standing driver-signing hurdles. The update improves installer reliability and kernel-mode VPN performance, underscoring how platform signing policies shape open-source tooling on Windows.</span><span class='zh'>在微软签名决议推动下，WireGuard 推出新的 Windows 版本，解决长期的驱动签名问题。更新提升安装可靠性与内核模式下的性能，体现了平台签名策略对开源工具在 Windows 上的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://words.filippo.io/crqc-timeline/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A cryptography engineer&#x27;s perspective on quantum computing timelines</span><span class='zh'>量子计算时间线：一位密码学工程师的观点</span></a><span class="dom">words.filippo.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A cryptography engineer offers a grounded view on quantum timelines, arguing that practical quantum attacks require far more qubits and error correction than currently available.</span><span class='zh'>一位密码学工程师对量子计算的时间线给出务实的看法，认为要实现实用的量子攻击仍需远多于现有的量子比特与纠错能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A cryptography engineer offers a grounded view on quantum timelines, arguing that practical quantum attacks require far more qubits and error correction than currently available. The piece stresses adopting post-quantum cryptography now and planning risk mitigation as a prudent long-term strategy.</span><span class='zh'>一位密码学工程师对量子计算的时间线给出务实的看法，认为要实现实用的量子攻击仍需远多于现有的量子比特与纠错能力。文章强调应尽早采用后量子密码并制定长期的风险缓解计划。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/04/faas-temporary-flight-restriction-drones-blatant-attempt-criminalize-filming-ice" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The FAA’s flight restriction for drones is an attempt to criminalize filming ICE</span><span class='zh'>FAA 的无人机飞行限制试图将拍摄 ICE 刑事化</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The FAA&#x27;s temporary drone flight restriction near certain facilities has drawn criticism for appearing to criminalize filming ICE activities and limiting First Amendment coverage of law enforcement.</span><span class='zh'>针对特定区域的无人机飞行限制引发担忧，批评者认为这等举措将拍摄 ICE 的行为刑事化并削弱对执法活动的监督报道。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The FAA&#x27;s temporary drone flight restriction near certain facilities has drawn criticism for appearing to criminalize filming ICE activities and limiting First Amendment coverage of law enforcement.</span><span class='zh'>针对特定区域的无人机飞行限制引发担忧，批评者认为这等举措将拍摄 ICE 的行为刑事化并削弱对执法活动的监督报道。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lemonade-server.ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lemonade by AMD: a fast and open source local LLM server using GPU and NPU</span><span class='zh'>AMD 的 Lemonade：一个使用 GPU/NPU 的快速开源本地 LLM 服务器</span></a><span class="dom">lemonade-server.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AMD&#x27;s Lemonade project showcases a fast, open-source local LLM server that uses both GPUs and NPUs to run offline, private inference.</span><span class='zh'>AMD 的 Lemonade 项目是一个快速、开源的本地大语言模型服务器，利用 GPU 和 NPU 实现离线私有推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AMD&#x27;s Lemonade project showcases a fast, open-source local LLM server that uses both GPUs and NPUs to run offline, private inference. By removing cloud dependencies, it aims to empower private AI workloads on consumer hardware, with implications for data control and customization.</span><span class='zh'>AMD 的 Lemonade 项目是一个快速、开源的本地大语言模型服务器，利用 GPU 和 NPU 实现离线私有推理。它让在本地硬件上运行强大模型成为可能，提升数据隐私与定制化能力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introducing Flipdiscs, a minimalist flipping-disk puzzle that challenges strategy with simple rules.</span><span class='zh'>介绍了 Flipdiscs 这款极简翻转圆盘的拼图游戏，靠简单规则考验策略。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introducing Flipdiscs, a minimalist flipping-disk puzzle that challenges strategy with simple rules. The post delves into core mechanics, potential AI challenges, and how small rule sets can yield deep, emergent gameplay. It’s a case study in design restraint and replayability.</span><span class='zh'>介绍了 Flipdiscs 这款极简翻转圆盘的拼图游戏，靠简单规则考验策略。文章探讨了核心机制、潜在的 AI 挑战，以及如何通过小规则集实现深度和高可玩性，成为设计克制与可重玩性的案例。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">182</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wesnoth.org" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Battle for Wesnoth: open-source, turn-based strategy game</span><span class='zh'>Wesnoth：开源的回合制策略游戏</span></a><span class="dom">wesnoth.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wesnoth is a long-running open-source turn-based strategy game with a fantasy setting and a large catalog of campaigns.</span><span class='zh'>Wesnoth 是一款长期由社区维护的开源回合制策略游戏，拥有丰富的战役和多人对战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wesnoth is a long-running open-source turn-based strategy game with a fantasy setting and a large catalog of campaigns. The project demonstrates how community-driven development can sustain a complex strategy title, with robust modding support and cross-platform availability. Its enduring viability highlights the strength of open-source ecosystems for niche, mechanics-heavy games.</span><span class='zh'>Wesnoth 是一款长期由社区维护的开源回合制策略游戏，拥有丰富的战役和多人对战。项目展示了开源社区如何支撑复杂策略游戏的持续开发，以及跨平台和可扩展的模组生态。它的长期活力凸显了开源生态在维护高复杂性游戏中的作用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>534</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664186' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>161</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47763759" data-id="47763759" data-tags="Programming" data-score="530" data-comments="465" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47763759" data-time="1776162819">
