<h3 class="st"><a href="https://steveklabnik.github.io/jujutsu-tutorial/introduction/what-is-jj-and-why-should-i-care.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>jj – the CLI for Jujutsu</span><span class='zh'>jj：Jujutsu 的命令行界面</span></a><span class="dom">steveklabnik.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>jj is a new command-line interface for Jujutsu, aiming to simplify interaction with the Jujutsu framework.</span><span class='zh'>jj 是一个新的 Jujutsu 命令行界面，旨在简化与 Jujutsu 框架的交互。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>jj is a new command-line interface for Jujutsu, aiming to simplify interaction with the Jujutsu framework. It provides a convenient, scriptable way to perform core tasks and experiments, reflecting a broader trend toward CLI-first tooling in niche systems.</span><span class='zh'>jj 是一个新的 Jujutsu 命令行界面，旨在简化与 Jujutsu 框架的交互。它提供一个便捷、可脚本化的方式来执行核心任务和实验，体现了在小众系统中向 CLI 优先工具的总体趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://cursor.com/blog/cursor-3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cursor 3</span><span class='zh'>Cursor 3 发布</span></a><span class="dom">cursor.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cursor 3 marks the latest release of the Cursor platform, bringing new features and performance enhancements.</span><span class='zh'>Cursor 3 作为该平台的最新版本，带来新特性与性能提升，体现出其软件工具生态的持续成熟与竞争力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cursor 3 marks the latest release of the Cursor platform, bringing new features and performance enhancements. The update demonstrates continued maturation and competitive positioning within its software tools ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>Cursor 3 作为该平台的最新版本，带来新特性与性能提升，体现出其软件工具生态的持续成熟与竞争力。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.dbreunig.com/2026/04/14/cybersecurity-is-proof-of-work-now.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.dbreunig.com/img/locks_and_keys.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='dbreunig.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.dbreunig.com/2026/04/14/cybersecurity-is-proof-of-work-now.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cybersecurity looks like proof of work now</span><span class='zh'>網路安全如今看起來像工作量證明</span></a><span class="dom">dbreunig.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cybersecurity is increasingly framed as a persistent, resource-intensive effort that mirrors proof-of-work dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>網路防護已成為一場持續且成本高昂的博弈，仿佛工作量證明在起作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cybersecurity is increasingly framed as a persistent, resource-intensive effort that mirrors proof-of-work dynamics. Defenders must continually invest to detect, deter, and recover from breaches, creating an ongoing arms race with threat actors. This perspective helps explain why security budgets tend to expand with rising threat capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>網路防護已成為一場持續且成本高昂的博弈，仿佛工作量證明在起作用。防禦方需要不斷投入資源來偵測、阻擊與修復破口，與威脅方的能力共同推動成本上升。以此觀點看待企業安全預算的成長具有說服力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>529</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769089' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>197</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47736476" data-id="47736476" data-tags="AI|Code" data-score="524" data-comments="400" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736476" data-time="1775972752">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic downgraded cache TTL on March 6th</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 在3月6日下调 Claude 的缓存 TTL</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic reduced Claude&#x27;s cache TTL on March 6, affecting how fresh responses are served to clients.</span><span class='zh'>3月6日，Anthropic 下调了 Claude 的缓存 TTL，导致返回结果的时效性和新鲜度受到影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic reduced Claude&#x27;s cache TTL on March 6, affecting how fresh responses are served to clients. The change increases cache churn, potentially improving freshness at the cost of latency and API throughput. It signals ongoing tuning of balance between speed, cost, and accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>3月6日，Anthropic 下调了 Claude 的缓存 TTL，导致返回结果的时效性和新鲜度受到影响。更高的缓存替换率提升了新鲜度，但可能增加延迟与成本，反映出在速度、成本与准确性之间的持续调参。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.inaturalist.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>iNaturalist</span><span class='zh'>iNaturalist 自然观测平台</span></a><span class="dom">inaturalist.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>iNaturalist enables crowdsourced biodiversity data from millions of users, driving research and conservation.</span><span class='zh'>iNaturalist 通过海量用户的群体观测，推动生物多样性研究与保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>iNaturalist enables crowdsourced biodiversity data from millions of users, driving research and conservation. Its open data catalog helps scientists track species distributions and ecological changes over time. The community-driven platform also highlights the value and challenges of citizen science data.</span><span class='zh'>iNaturalist 通过海量用户的群体观测，推动生物多样性研究与保护。开放数据目录帮助科学家跟踪物种分布与生态变化，提升研究与教育的可及性。社区驱动的模式同时也暴露了公民科学数据的质量与治理挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Overthinking, scope creep, and frequent structural diffs create drag on projects, leading to delays and quality issues.</span><span class='zh'>本文分析过度思考、范围蠕变和频繁的结构性变动如何拖慢项目、造成延迟和质量下降。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Overthinking, scope creep, and frequent structural diffs create drag on projects, leading to delays and quality issues.</span><span class='zh'>本文分析过度思考、范围蠕变和频繁的结构性变动如何拖慢项目、造成延迟和质量下降。提供对策：明确范围、记录决策、保持稳定接口、采用小步迭代并快速获取反馈。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The funding round valued the company at $852 billion, signaling strong investor confidence in AI momentum.</span><span class='zh'>此次融资使 OpenAI 的估值达到 8,520 亿美元，显示市场对 AI 领域的强烈投资热情。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Trump fired the National Science Foundation&#x27;s independent oversight board, a move that reshapes how the agency is governed and funded.</span><span class='zh'>特朗普解雇了国家科学基金会的独立监督委员会，此举将重塑机构治理与拨款流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Trump fired the National Science Foundation&#x27;s independent oversight board, a move that reshapes how the agency is governed and funded. Critics warn it reduces external accountability, while supporters say it could speed decision-making on grants and programs.</span><span class='zh'>特朗普解雇了国家科学基金会的独立监督委员会，此举将重塑机构治理与拨款流程。批评者认为这削弱了外部问责，支持者则称有助于加速资助与项目的决策。</span></p>

The feat highlights advances in training, pacing, and data-driven preparation enabled by modern sports tech.</span><span class='zh'>据称萨韦在正式比赛中首次实现马拉松两小时内的里程碑，挑战人类极限。此成就反映出训练、 pacing 与数据驱动的备战在当代体育科技中的进步。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nationaltoday.com/us/tx/austin/news/2026/04/03/oracle-files-thousands-of-h-1b-visa-petitions-amid-mass-layoffs/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Oracle files H-1B visa petitions amid mass layoffs</span><span class='zh'>Oracle 在大规模裁员期间提交成千上万份 H-1B 签证申请</span></a><span class="dom">nationaltoday.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Oracle filed thousands of H-1B visa petitions even as it cut workers, underscoring how tech giants balance layoffs with access to specialized talent.</span><span class='zh'>Oracle 在裁员的同时提交数千份 H-1B 签证申请，凸显巨头在裁员与获取专业人才之间的权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Oracle filed thousands of H-1B visa petitions even as it cut workers, underscoring how tech giants balance layoffs with access to specialized talent. The filings may reflect plans to replace departing staff for critical projects or to maintain a ready pipeline of skilled engineers, drawing attention to immigration policies in the tech industry.</span><span class='zh'>Oracle 在裁员的同时提交数千份 H-1B 签证申请，凸显巨头在裁员与获取专业人才之间的权衡。相关申请或用于替换离职员工、保障关键项目的人才储备，引发对科技行业移民政策的关注。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores factors behind Japan&#x27;s top-tier rail network: meticulous maintenance, on-time performance, dense urban planning, and integrated operations.</span><span class='zh'>本文分析日本铁路的四大支撑：精细维护、准点运营、密集的城市规划以及一体化运营。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores factors behind Japan&#x27;s top-tier rail network: meticulous maintenance, on-time performance, dense urban planning, and integrated operations. The piece shows how culture, funding, and technology converge to deliver reliable service.</span><span class='zh'>本文分析日本铁路的四大支撑：精细维护、准点运营、密集的城市规划以及一体化运营。作者指出文化、资金投入与技术协同共同铸就了高水平的铁路服务。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://boringbar.app/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: boringBar – a taskbar-style dock replacement for macOS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：boringBar——macOS 的任务栏风格 Dock 替代</span></a><span class="dom">boringbar.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>boringBar is a macOS replacement for the Dock with a taskbar-style interface.</span><span class='zh'>boringBar 是一个为 macOS 设计的 Dock 替代，采用类似任务栏的界面，便于固定应用、快速启动并在各工作区保持一致。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>boringBar is a macOS replacement for the Dock with a taskbar-style interface. It emphasizes persistent pinned apps, quick-launch, and consistent shortcuts across spaces, offering an alternative UX to Apple’s native Dock. If it gains traction, it signals demand for more modular, multitasking-oriented macOS UI.</span><span class='zh'>boringBar 是一个为 macOS 设计的 Dock 替代，采用类似任务栏的界面，便于固定应用、快速启动并在各工作区保持一致。它为多任务工作提供了新的交互方式，若受欢迎，可能反映出对更模块化、以多任务为导向的 macOS UI 的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/changes-to-github-copilot-individual-plans/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Changes to GitHub Copilot individual plans</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Copilot 个人计划调整</span></a><span class="dom">github.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub Copilot’s individual plans have changed, with updated pricing and terms affecting solo developers and hobbyists.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Copilot 个人计划发生变动，定价与条款更新，影响单独开发者和爱好者。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub Copilot’s individual plans have changed, with updated pricing and terms affecting solo developers and hobbyists. The move highlights GitHub’s ongoing strategy to monetize AI-assisted coding tools and could shift how individuals adopt the service.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Copilot 个人计划发生变动，定价与条款更新，影响单独开发者和爱好者。此举折射出 GitHub 在 AI 助力编码工具上的持续商业化策略，可能改变个人用户的采用路径。</span></p>
